Having barely evaded, I unleashed the sword-qi release technique, the Fourth Form, Flaming Heaven.

The sword qi flew forth at terrifying speed, striking Seophon Majon with a heavy thud. He staggered greatly as if about to fall, but steadied himself. A part of him that should have been cleanly severed remained intact.

A stream of black liquid flowed from the corner of Seophon Majon’s mouth, yet he still looked unyielding.

“Yi Gongja, why do you not wield the Guhwa Demon Art?”

Seophon Majon’s voice had changed into a metallic rasp, no longer sounding human. It meant he was merging with the Dark Demon Art he had learned. At this rate, he would eventually be consumed by the demonic art. Such was the way of powerful demonic arts—they always demand a price.

Even so, he was pushing himself beyond his limits, and I was in just as much danger.

“Because I thought I could defeat one such as you even without using the Guhwa Demon Art.”

It was a deliberate provocation. In a fight where the slightest edge decides life and death, the moment you lose your composure, you lose your life. And this provocation had another aim as well.

“Your arrogance will eventually lead you to death...”

The moment Seophon Majon began to speak, the Soaring Heaven Sword Art’s finest swift-sword technique, the Fifth Form, Azure Heaven, was unleashed. It was a decisive attack meant to sever the breath of a speaking foe.

Azure Heaven precisely split the bastard’s throat. But his neck was not severed; only black liquid trickled out.

He didn’t even get his neck cut by Azure Heaven?

“You have truly become a monster.”

At my words, Seophon Majon charged at me. He seemed to erupt in fury, but his breathing remained steady. The taunt tactic did not work on him.

Aiming for a chance to unleash my ultimate final technique, we soared into the sky.

Even as we rose, sword clashed endlessly against sword. Like two dragons ascending as they coiled around each other, we exchanged blades in an entangling dance.

Invisible to the eye, only the sounds of swords and the radiance of blade light filled that space. That Seophon Majon, known only for his Soul-Reaping Technique, would hide such a demonic art as his final trump card—this place was the martial world.

At the end of that brilliant melee, the instant we separated from each other, Seophon Majon’s gaze blazed pure white.

‘Danger!’

Seophon Majon unleashed his final move. His roar erupted in a perfect metallic screech!

“Heavenly Annihilation!”

I did not evade. His opportunity was also my opportunity. We saw the same opening, and we sent our blades toward that gap.

Together with the Bright King Step, I poured all of my internal energy into the Soaring Heaven Sword Art’s Eighth Form, Yellow Spring.

Shhhhhhheeeeek!

Shhhhraaaaaack!

Two lights intersected.

Radiant sword light painted the side where I stood. Space fractured into ninety-six pieces, and an entire plane was sheared away. It was truly a spectacle worthy of awe.

Seophon Majon did not look at the awesome spectacle he had created. He was looking down at his own heart.

Blood flowed from the heart pierced by my Black Demon Sword. Not black liquid, but red blood. His massive heart, which had been pulsing powerfully, had ceased to move.

“How... in the world...?”

To his disbelieving face, I replied calmly.

“Your heart was so large, it proved easy to pierce.”

Why else? I said it because his greed in robbing the innocent of their hearts had brought him this very end today.

Black smoke leaked from his body, and his transformation began to break.

He, who had been more monstrous than an actual monster, transformed back into an ordinary person—the kind you might save ten of in Magachon.

“...It wasn’t the Guhwa Demon Art?”

“It was the union of the Bright King Step and the Soaring Heaven Sword Art’s Eighth Form.”

“The Bright King Step?”

“The Bright King Step of the Wind God Steps.”

“How do you have the Wind God Steps?”

“Perhaps Heaven arranged it so that I would not die by your hand.”

It was no empty word. Had it not been for the Wind God Steps, I would have died in today’s battle. I realized it keenly: one must never let one’s guard down when facing a Demon Lord.

To be honest, I had been confident I could defeat him because I had obtained the Blood Eye Essence.

But he had possessed a hidden trump card. A truly perilous final move that would have killed me, had it not been for the Wind God Steps.

Hyeolcheon Doma, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, and the other remaining Demon Lords as well—they would surely have hidden moves different from what they show.

Today, I have grown stronger.

Not because I killed Seophon Majon, but because I will never let my guard down again. That is why I have grown stronger.

Seophon Majon slowly crumbled.

As he died, the space he had created began to crack.

The life faded from his black pupils, which had dilated and contracted so violently. He looked up at me and spoke with difficulty.

“...If I am reborn, I will surely kill you then.”

I did not respond with a curse.

“If you are reborn, live a long, long life then. There will surely be better ways than this.”

At my words, his gaze wavered. He tried to say something, but it never made it past his lips. It might have been an even more terrible curse, or perhaps he had tried to convey his final regret.

Seophon Majon lowered his head and perished. At the same time, the world he had created vanished.

We had returned to the place where we had first attempted to carry out the Heart-Soul Great Method.

Even in death, Seophon Majon presented a final mystery.

Seophon Majon sat dead in a meditative posture. Though we had fought like madmen, there was not a single external wound, as if he had died from qi deviation. He had died in the world he had loved so dearly.

I took the child lying on the altar into my arms and quietly left that place.

* * *

I bought good wine and side dishes and returned to Hyeolcheon Doma.

I entrusted the child to Seo Daeryong, telling him to find the parents secretly. Since I told him it was a child who had nearly been sacrificed for the great method, the quick-witted and clever Seo Daeryong would handle it secretly on his own.

“Why did it take so long? Did you really make the side dishes yourself?”

“How did you know?”

“What?”

“Making the side dishes took some time.”

“What nonsense is this? What on earth did you make?”

Hyeolcheon Doma looked puzzled when he saw the dishes I had bought.

“These aren’t dishes that take long to make.”

I smiled and poured wine for Hyeolcheon Doma.

“Here, have a drink. The night is still long.”

“Do you think an old man’s stamina is like yours? If I stay up all night, I’ll be drowsy all day.”

“But you’re still full of vigor, aren’t you? Come, let’s enjoy ourselves today.”

“Yi Gongja, you seem to be in a good mood today.”

“Yes, I’m feeling good.”

“Why?”

“It’s just good to see you after so long, sir.”

Hyeolcheon Doma looked at me with eyes that wondered what I was up to.

But how could he know? That I had killed Seophon Majon while I was away.

Meeting again after a long time, we drank several cups in succession. Since meeting him, this was the first time I had drunk so refreshingly and pleasantly.

We shared many stories. About the state of the martial world, about martial arts, and about Seo Daeryong and the Sword Venerable.

Others might find it unexpected, but both of us were talkative, so the conversation at the drinking table never ran dry.

“Do you know why I took a liking to Jwibangul?”

“Why?”

“On the first day, I asked him. Whether he could lay down his life for you. He said he couldn’t.”

“That rascal, I’ll have to scold him.”

“I liked his honesty then.”

“Please tell Inspector Seo directly. He will probably be pleased.”

“Don’t bother.”

“Then I will tell him next time. Someone has to say these things. A single word can change a person’s life, after all. Why are you looking at me like that?”

“You really take care of all sorts of people. Next you’ll be looking after the stable boy mucking out horse dung. Why, why don’t you go feed the horses with him?”

“If I could, wouldn’t that be a good thing?”

“I have never seen anyone like you in my life.”

“The Cult must be the same. It will become a Heavenly Demon Divine Cult the likes of which no one has ever seen. Wouldn’t it be fine to have such a Cult for a generation?”

Hyeolcheon Doma stared at me. The gaze he fixed on me was profound, as if weighing his thoughts.

“Yi Gongja.”

“Yes.”

“Pour me a drink.”

I had poured him drinks every time until now. But this time, the feeling was different from usual.

I could feel it. That this drink I was pouring now held a different meaning from the others.

So I said to him.

“I pour the first drink for my Left Emissary.”

He could have said something like, “What nonsense is this?” but he silently accepted the drink. I filled the cup to the brim.

Hyeolcheon Doma stared at that cup of wine for a long while.

And then he emptied it.

Perhaps in this moment, he had made up his mind to truly follow me.

Of course, it was only a guess. He was truly an unpredictable man.

Then, Doma suddenly spoke.

“Is the Left Emissary high, or is the Right Emissary high? If you’re going to give me a title, give me the higher one.”

I stared at him blankly for a moment, then burst into laughter.

“Hahahaha.”

We drank through the night.

Even relationships that seem so good can go awry because of one misspoken word. That is why you must keep building them. Until they become a solid bond that won’t be shaken by one or two mistakes. Whether it’s drinks together, a brawl, a gift, a cautious piece of advice, or even praise you don’t mean. Whatever it is, you must forge and pile and pile again. Because both he and I can make mistakes.

“Looking at you today, you are handsome.”

“You crazy bastard.”

Hyeolcheon Doma raised his Heaven-Annihilating Great Saber, reflecting our faces in its blade.

“Here, look clearly at my face and say that again.”

“Hahaha.”

“Why are you just laughing! Hurry up and tell me I’m handsome!”

“I suppose one shouldn’t confess to a woman when drunk.”

Hyeolcheon Doma laughed, saying he’d caught a sycophant. I laughed along with him.

For the fortune of gaining a true follower is surely granted to someone among those who have made at least this much effort.

Chapter 57: May No Other Monsters Appear.
-----------------------------------------
The next day, news that the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had died struck the cult.

Many people had died of late, but the death of a Demon Venerable was an entirely different matter. An emergency was declared within the cult, and a full-scale investigation began.

The ones dispatched to investigate the incident were our Hwangcheon Pavilion. Given the gravity of the matter, every special investigator and twenty enforcement martial artists went to Seohwanjin.

In front of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s residence, his disciples and ghost-arts practitioners had taken up positions.

“You cannot enter.”

They absolutely refused to let us in. They were flustered and had no idea how to handle the situation. Until the other Demon Venerables made some decision, their first instinct was to block our entry.

I did not bother clashing with them.

“Very well. Then at least let Elder Demon Physician in. The corpse must be treated so it does not decay, and shouldn’t the precise cause of death be determined?”

The Demon Physician was known as a politically neutral figure, and since every member of the cult trusted and respected him, they did not refuse that suggestion.

After a long and meticulous examination, the Demon Physician came out and spoke to me and to the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s disciples.

“It appears he died after excessively releasing his internal energy on his own, causing severe damage to his heart and the blood vessels throughout his body. There are no traces of intrusion or combat nearby, and he was not poisoned by any deadly toxin, so it seems he died after falling into qi deviation.”

At those words, the disciples sighed in grief, while I rejoiced inwardly.

Now that the words qi deviation had come from the Demon Physician’s mouth, we could avoid having to search for a culprit.

On the way out of Seohwanjin and toward the Heavenly Demon Hall to report, Seo Daeryong asked cautiously.

“……It wasn’t, right?”

“What wasn’t?”

“You know. That.”

Perhaps he could not bring himself to say it aloud, because Seo Daeryong sent a sound transmission.

—Pavilion Lord, you didn’t kill the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, did you?

—Why? Do you think I killed him?

—……Yesterday, you brought that child to me, didn’t you? The child who nearly became a sacrifice to the Mind-Soul Grand Art.

And that was not all. Two months ago, when we left the cult, he had told me I had the look in my eyes of someone trying to make the impossible possible. Now that impossibility had become possible and appeared as reality.

—Do you really want to know?

—No, no! I would rather not know. I’ll return ahead of you.

Seo Daeryong turned his steps toward Hwangcheon Pavilion and walked away.

In the distance, the Heavenly Demon Hall came into view.

How would Father react?

* * *

I slowly moved my feet along the Path of Blood.

As always, Father was seated in the grand preceptor’s chair. Today, the Chief Strategist, Sama Myeong, was with him as well.

After I greeted the two of them, Sama Myeong asked me,

“Second Young Master, how was your trip outside the cult?”

“The work was just an excuse. I had a good time.”

“You look even better than before.”

“Strategist, you should stop working all the time and get some fresh air. Slack off a little.”

“The one day I decide to play after a lifetime of not doing so is usually the day our enemies invade.”

At Sama Myeong’s joke, we smiled together.

After finishing our greetings with such light banter, I reported on the death of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

“The Demon Physician concluded that it was qi deviation.”

At my words, Father and Sama Myeong said nothing for a moment. I could feel it. Neither of them believed the result. What were the odds that someone who was a Demon Venerable would suddenly fall into qi deviation and die?

So I struck first.

“It is hard to believe that someone of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s caliber fell to qi deviation. There is also the possibility that he was murdered by someone.”

“That possibility exists.”

“Hwangcheon Pavilion will investigate the incident.”

“This matter will be investigated by the Demon Venerables.”

“Not by us?”

“By tradition, the Heavenly Demon Hall cannot intervene in matters concerning the death of a Demon Venerable. We have no choice but to watch everything unfold.”

“Well, if that is the case, I will not step forward. It is easier and better for us.”

After we exchanged a few more words regarding this incident, Sama Myeong naturally excused himself.

Once Sama Myeong had withdrawn, Father asked bluntly,

“How did you kill him?”

“What do you mean?”

“How did you kill Seophon?”

Father’s emotion was not anger, but curiosity. He was not someone who lingered over the dead. He was probably thinking something like this:

How on earth did this brat kill Seophon? And with qi deviation as the cause of death, no less.

“At times like this, I would like to give you a suitably impressive answer. That I did this and that, and killed him in such a way. But you are overestimating me. No matter how talented I may be, I am not so extraordinary that I could make the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable fall into qi deviation and die.”

In truth, I wanted to be honest with Father.

But there was no way to explain how I had obtained the Blood-Eye Essence. At least the fact that the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had fallen into qi deviation and died was truly fortunate.

If he had died in battle rather than from qi deviation, they would have thoroughly analyzed the cause of death and tried first to determine who had killed him. In that case, I would have had to bury the corpse somewhere. At any rate, it would have been far more troublesome than it was now in many ways.

“Yesterday, I drank with Elder Blade Demon all night. He can vouch for my whereabouts.”

But Father snorted.

“Did the two of you go together and kill him?”

“No. Absolutely not. Father, this was heaven’s punishment. The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was a monster who dug out people’s hearts while they were still alive.”

In any case, Father believed I had killed him.

Even so, there was another reason I did not tell the truth.

It was a truth that would burden Father. It would put him in a position where he knew I had killed a Demon Venerable and still had to turn a blind eye.

After looking down at me in silence for a moment, Father suddenly said,

“Now that Seophon is dead, even the Demon Venerables who had been keeping quiet will move.”

The Eight Demon Venerables were bound together in life and death. The death of one meant a threat to them all. Just as Father suspected me, they too might suspect me.

I looked at Father and said calmly,

“I only hope that another monster does not emerge in the process.”

* * *

After leaving the Heavenly Demon Hall, the place I went to was the Northern Heaven Sword Clan.

The one who received me was Sa Ujong.

“Welcome, Second Young Master.”

He greeted me with a brighter expression than usual.

‘Sa Ujong, Sa Ujong. Do you know that, strictly speaking, you were the one who killed the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable?’

If he had not drawn in the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, I would never have learned about the Mind-Soul Grand Art.

The person most shocked and flustered by this situation would be Sa Ujong himself. I should have died, yet the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had died instead.

After guiding me to the thatched cottage, he bowed politely and turned away.

“Then please speak at your leisure.”

He was certainly not a good man, but that did not mean I could punish him for having drawn in the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

I intended to pretend not to know and keep an eye on him, then use him in turn if an opportunity arose.

“Welcome, Second Young Master.”

In the past, the One Blossom Sword Venerable had not even looked back at me and had only tended to her flower garden. Now, she greeted me gladly.

“Have you been well?”

“I heard you left the cult. Did your trip go well?”

“Yes. I got some fresh air and returned.”

“Every time I see you, Second Young Master, you have advanced by leaps and bounds.”

“Do I seem different?”

Was it because of our three-day bout? She was certainly sensitive to changes in me.

“So much so that I feel the urge to request a bout with you right away.”

“Let us arrange one soon.”

“Good. You are welcome anytime.”

I held out what I had brought for her.

“This is a modest gift for you, Senior.”

What I had brought were seeds that were difficult to obtain in this region.

“I brought them because I heard they bloom into flowers with a fine fragrance.”

“Oh, this is the first time in my life I have received seeds as a gift.”

“It is my first time giving them as well.”

“Thank you. I will raise them well. Now, come in and have some tea.”

For the first time, I entered her home.

The place was elegantly furnished and smelled pleasant.

“Your home is cozy and nice.”

“Nice? It is nothing special, just a place where an old woman lives alone.”

“Old? Walk down the streets of Hangzhou with me just once. Men would probably come up every ten steps and try to coax you into drinking with them.”

“What nonsense!”

Though she waved her hand dismissively, she could not hide her smile. She was weaker to compliments like this than to praise of her outstanding swordsmanship.

“Let us go sometime. Together with Elder Blade Demon.”

At the mention of the Blade Demon, her expression stiffened slightly. She still had a poor relationship with him. She simply tried not to show it as much as possible because I was between them. Reconciling her with the Blade Demon was still no easy task.

She naturally changed the subject.

“Did you hear that the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable died?”

“Yes, I heard.”

“In that death…… were you involved?”

As she said that, she stared intently at me.

At that moment, her gaze was cold. Apart from the goodwill she held toward me, the death of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was something that put the other Demon Venerables on edge.

The awareness that if one fell, all might fall was their rule of survival, passed down for a very long time.

“The Demon Physician concluded that he died from qi deviation.”

“Not a single Demon Venerable will believe that.”

“Even so, it was not me.”

At my firm words, the One Blossom Sword Venerable withdrew the suspicion from her eyes, but I could not know whether she had withdrawn it from her heart as well.

“Why did you think it was me?”

It was probably because of our bout. She knew my skill was nearly equal to hers.

“Because the lake is rippling from the waves you have caused. Ah, you said you preferred the stormy sea to a lake, did you not? The moment you immersed yourself in it, the once-peaceful lake had already become the sea. The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was the first to drown in that sea.”

“I am someone who fishes in that sea. I do not push people in and drown them.”

“That may be true. You did fish up the Blade Demon, after all.”

“Would you be difficult, Senior?”

“It might be possible if you released the fish you have already caught. I do not wish to enter the net where the Blade Demon is being held.”

I smiled faintly, and she did not smile.

“You can never know how matters between people will turn out. The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was a man who treasured and protected himself so fiercely…… Yet to think he would be the first to die like this.”

“Seohwanjin will be in turmoil for the time being.”

“There will be a fight over succession.”

When a Demon Venerable died, a successor inherited the position, and if four of the remaining seven Demon Venerables acknowledged that person, they became the successor.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had five disciples. Since he had died without formally choosing a successor, the most outstanding among those five would inherit the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s position.

“Whom do you support?”

“Why are you curious about that?”

“If possible, would it not be better for someone on our side to take over?”

“Our side?”

“Yes, our side. We are sparring friends, after all.”

I struck first before she could think of the Blade Demon.

Perhaps the phrase sparring friends amused her, for only then did she smile faintly.

“I can share the same purpose as a sparring friend.”

After exchanging a few more words with her, I took my leave.

“I will be going now for today.”

“Thank you for coming despite being busy.”

The One Blossom Sword Venerable followed me out to the yard.

“Second Young Master. When you fish, some fish are caught easily, while others hold fast beneath the water. But be careful. Some fish may leap out and attack the fisherman, and some may even overturn the boat.”

In short, she was telling me to beware of the other Eight Demon Venerables, aside from the Blade Demon and herself.

“Thank you for your concern.”

“And do not forget. I am not yet a fish that has been caught.”

“The fact that you offer me such advice—does that not mean you are half caught already?”

The One Blossom Sword Venerable did not seem particularly offended by the notion that she was half caught. Perhaps she herself acknowledged that it was exactly to that extent.

“Then please take care until we meet again.”

After leaving the thatched cottage and walking a short distance, I looked back to see her in the yard, planting the seeds I had given her in the soil. It felt like only yesterday she had glared at me fiercely, and already we had come to have such a relationship.

She was right. You truly never knew how matters between people would turn out.

As Father and the One Blossom Sword Venerable had warned, the very same day, the seven Demon Venerables held a meeting.

It was a special meeting arranged because of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s death. Since it was a meeting attended only by the Demon Venerables, not even Father knew what they discussed.

On the night of the meeting, the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon came to see me. His expression was not good.

“Do you have any liquor?”

Chapter 58: Don't Kill Them for Now.
------------------------------------
The Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked for wine the moment he sat down.

I brought out the wine I had at home and filled a cup for him.

“Has something unpleasant happened?”

Only after downing several cups in succession did the Blood Heaven Blade Demon reveal why he was angry.

“Those crippled fools were openly excluding me.”

“Hahaha.”

“I did not say that to make you laugh.”

“My apologies. I simply never imagined there would be a Demon Venerable who was being ostracized.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon glared even harder, as if to say he was not joking.

“I’m sorry. How exactly did they ostracize you?”

“No one spoke to me.”

“Wasn’t that how it usually was?”

“They never talked much, but not like today. I’d rather they had confronted me outright. Then I wouldn’t be this angry.”

“Confronted you about what?”

“You, of course. You!”

“Pardon? Me?”

“They suspect you conspired with me to kill the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable. That’s why they’re excluding me.”

“Did we do that?”

“We did not.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon stared at me with cold eyes.

“You must have. You killed him, didn’t you?”

“Why does everyone suspect me?”

“Because you’re the one who has stained his hands with the most blood lately. There is no law saying Soul-Capturing’s blood cannot be on those hands as well, is there? It was you, wasn’t it?”

Instead of answering, I gazed at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. He must have read it in my eyes—that I had killed the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

“Why are you telling me the truth?”

“I said nothing.”

“True, not in words. You said it with your eyes.”

If I had intended to deny it flatly, I could have. There were two reasons I did not.

The first was to make him feel a sense of tension toward me. Friendship was friendship, but he and I would be bound by the relationship of lord and retainer. It was meant to tell him not to harbor other thoughts, but to trust and follow me. In human relationships, a proper degree of tension reduces mistakes.

The second reason was that I trusted him. I wanted to tell him the truth, and I believed that even if I did, no problems would arise.

“You truly trust me.”

“If I did not, I would never have seated you as my Left Envoy.”

“Before I am your Left Envoy, I am one of the Eight Demon Venerables.”

“You are one of the Eight Demon Venerables, but you are also my Left Envoy.”

“If you trust people this easily, one day you will be stabbed in the back.”

“It was not easy trust. Think of everything that has happened between you and me.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon abruptly asked,

“Are you not afraid?”

“Of what?”

“Of making enemies of the Eight Demon Venerables.”

“To be precise, it would be the Four Demon Venerables.”

“The Four Demon Venerables?”

After staring at me blankly for a moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon slammed his cup onto the table and sprang to his feet.

“What? When did you kill three more? Who did you kill?”

I hurriedly spoke to him as he roared.

“Calm yourself. That is not what I meant.”

It was only for a very brief instant, but the fact that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had actually thought that way surprised me. Just how highly did this man think of me?

“Then what is this talk about the Four Demon Venerables?”

“Listen to my calculation. Since the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable is dead, there are now seven Demon Venerables, correct? Remove you, and that leaves six. The Sword Venerable has only come over to me halfway, so five and a half Demon Venerables. Let us say, for now, that five and a half Demon Venerables are my enemies. In this situation, you and Senior Sword Venerable would not simply sit around doing nothing, would you? If the two of you together take on one and a half Demon Venerables, then exactly four Demon Venerables remain. That is why I said my opponents were the Four Demon Venerables. In fact, it may be even fewer. If just two more among the seven Demon Venerables join hands with me, I only have to face two Demon Venerables. Easy, isn’t it?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at me with the most dumbfounded expression he had worn since we first met.

“Fine. Since I already know you are a madman to this extent, I will let that pass. But there is one thing you have failed to consider.”

“What is it?”

“Neither I nor the Sword Venerable will ever fight the Demon Venerables.”

I knew that as well. It was an ironclad rule among the Demon Venerables that had been handed down for a very long time.

“I know. I will do the fighting, so all you need to do is not betray me.”

“Betrayal is not done by people. It is done by circumstances. So you must not trust me.”

“Yes, I will not create circumstances in which I can be betrayed. So please help me, Elder.”

I treated the Blood Heaven Blade Demon with trust. I believed that was the best way to make him my man.

After drinking, he spoke as if pouring it all out.

“One wrong move and you die, you bastard!”

In that instant, my chest stirred. From the words he had barked out, I felt sincere concern.

“Yes. I will be careful.”

We drank without another word. When his cup emptied, I filled it, and we drank again. When the wine bottle was nearly empty, he told me one thing.

I had said before that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was more emotional than I was. At the time, he denied it, but he was certainly an emotional man. If he were not, he would not be telling me this now.

“The Demon Venerables have called someone in to uncover the truth behind this incident.”

“Who is it?”

An unexpected person came from his mouth.

“The Fengtian Cult Leader.”

I was startled. The Fengtian Cult Leader, of all people? It was none other than the Fengtian Cult where I had obtained the Yin Thunder Bell and the Blood-Eyed Essence.

“Why the Fengtian Cult Leader?”

“Because Soul-Capturing’s sorcery is based on the demonic arts of the Blood Cult. As you well know, the Blood Cult was the predecessor of the Fengtian Cult. So they judged that if the Fengtian Cult Leader examines Soul-Capturing’s corpse, he will be able to determine the exact cause of death.”

“I heard the Fengtian Cult Leader never vacates his seat of power.”

“He will certainly come.”

“Why are you so certain?”

“Because they will make him come, one way or another. Do not underestimate the power of the Eight Demon Venerables. They will ensure the Fengtian Cult Leader comes here no matter what. Whether by threats, by handing him treasures, or by some other means.”

In the past, during the era of the Blood Cult, such a thing would have been impossible. They had fought several wars with our cult and were a force that could not coexist with us.

But afterward, when they became the Fengtian Cult, the relationship changed. As the Eight Demon Venerables joined hands with them, the Fengtian Cult established itself as an ally that was not quite an ally.

“What about the sacred relics of the Fengtian Cult?”

“He will handle it. If he is that anxious, he will come even if he has to pack them all up and bring them with him.”

“Wouldn’t it be better to send the corpse there instead?”

There was also the method of treating it so it would not decay and sending it by carriage.

“The Fengtian Cult Leader is not coming here solely for the autopsy.”

“There is another reason?”

“There are three more. The first is that the Demon Venerables are showing their will to find the culprit no matter what. They intend to show not only our cult, but even the Martial Alliance and the Evil Dao Alliance, that the Eight Demon Venerables must never be touched. That is the most important purpose.”

“They are trying to show that they can move even the Fengtian Cult Leader. That if anyone touches a Demon Venerable, they will be avenged without fail.”

“Exactly.”

“What is the second reason?”

“The Cult Leader.”

“My father?”

“They are showing the Cult Leader. They are flaunting the fact that the reason the Fengtian Cult and the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult are currently allies is not because of the Cult Leader, but because of the Eight Demon Venerables.”

The Eight Demon Venerables had checked my father endlessly. Perhaps the reason my father had drawn me in was also born from that conflict.

“And the third reason?”

“You.”

“Me?”

“If you killed the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, the Fengtian Cult Leader will surely uncover that fact through you. He will figure out what sort of person you are and determine what method you used to kill him.”

He was right. If it was the Fengtian Cult Leader, he might even discover that I had placed the Blood-Eyed Essence in my eyes.

I absolutely had to hide the fact that I had killed the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable. If it came to light, never mind the revenge the Eight Demon Venerables would take on me; with their desperate opposition, I would never be able to become the successor.

“By the way, are you all right?”

“Me? Why would I not be?”

“You became my accomplice, did you not? Since you drank with me all night, they may think we moved together.”

“Perhaps I dozed off for a moment while drinking.”

At his words that he would wriggle out of it like that if necessary, I gave a faint laugh.

“Which of the Eight Demon Venerables went to fetch him?”

“How did you know one of the Eight Demon Venerables went?”

“Of course someone important would have gone. Would it even be strange if all of them went together?”

“The Demon Buddha went.”

The Demon Buddha, one of the Eight Demon Venerables, was close to the Fengtian Cult Leader. Sending him meant they were determined to bring the Fengtian Cult Leader back.

Smiling, I asked the Blood Heaven Blade Demon,

“Shall I chase after him and kill the Demon Buddha? Or kill the Fengtian Cult Leader as he sets out? Or perhaps…… kill them both?”

“If you do that, we will end up at war with the martial world beyond the borders. If we split apart, those idiots in the Martial Alliance will be quiet one moment and suddenly start shouting about destroying the Demon Cult. As for the Evil Dao Alliance, their eyes will turn bloodshot looking for scraps to pick up.”

“Let us drench ourselves in blood together, just once.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon stood up and said,

“If you wish to die, die alone.”

“Elder, were you and I not to live and die together?”

“Second Young Master, from the moment we were born, each of us has had to seek his own survival. I am leaving.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon left my room.

I drained the last of the wine.

‘The Fengtian Cult Leader is coming, is he?’

* * *

The next morning, when I arrived at the Heavenly Demon Hall after being summoned by my father, he was not seated in the grand chair but standing by the window.

“You called for me, Father.”

“Come here.”

I slowly climbed the stairs and walked to where my father stood.

“I hear Seohwanjin is already in an uproar.”

“Would ghosts be any different? The power struggle must be beginning.”

Seohwanjin, which had lost its master overnight, was in chaos.

At the center of that chaos, there was no grief. Because the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had always been immersed solely in his own world, no one mourned his death. The spirit sorcerers were focused only on who would become the next Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

“Go out for a while.”

“Where do you mean?”

“Is it not about time you toured the branches of the Yellow Springs Pavilion in the Central Plains?”

I could tell. My father also knew that the Eight Demon Venerables had called the Fengtian Cult Leader. He was trying to send me outside because he was worried about me.

“Please reconsider sending me away.”

“Why?”

“The Eight Demon Venerables have called the Fengtian Cult Leader to investigate the death of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.”

As expected, my father was not surprised in the least. He already knew. For a moment, I thought that perhaps one of the Demon Venerables was among my father’s eyes and limbs. Or else a highly skilled spy was relaying precise information about the movements of the Eight Demon Venerables.

“If I leave the cult at a time like this, they will suspect me.”

“They already suspect you. That is why they called the Fengtian Cult Leader. The Fengtian Cult Leader is not an easy man. I do not know what method you used to kill Soul-Capturing, but he will certainly reveal that it was your doing.”

“It was not me, Father. Could the Martial Alliance not have sent an assassin?”

“That would mean they intend to wage war against us, and they are far from war at the moment.”

The Martial Alliance and we knew each other inside and out. If war were to break out, no matter how secretly they proceeded, we would detect the signs at least three days in advance.

“Are you truly confident you can get through this matter without incident?”

“Yes.”

“Where does that confidence come from?”

He had asked the same question before. I gave the same answer in a different way.

“If you were me, Father, would you leave the cult?”

“No.”

“I am your son.”

“Yes, you are my son, not me.”

“But I can at least imitate you. If the subject of imitation is you, Father, then imitation alone is enough.”

My father decided he could no longer break my will.

“Very well. Then keep one thing in mind.”

I thought he was going to tell me to be careful. Naturally, I expected as much.

As my father turned and walked away, he said,

“You must not kill the Fengtian Cult Leader.”

At that moment, I realized.

“Surely not? You have not been worried about me all this time, but about the Fengtian Cult Leader?”

As my father strode out, he added,

“Not any other Demon Venerable either! For the time being, do not kill anyone.”

“I told you, I did not kill him!”

I denied it to the very end, but my father had already closed the door and left.

I slowly descended the stairs, walked the path of blood, and left the Heavenly Demon Hall.

Kill the Fengtian Cult Leader? I know the Fengtian Cult and its leader far too well for that. To me, he is someone as welcome as a treasure vault. However, if he comes all the way here and pressures me…… then he will become someone even more welcome to me.

Chapter 59: Sometimes the Shell Matters More Than the Inside.
-------------------------------------------------------------
As far as I know, there are two ways to avoid being found out after putting Blood-Eye Essence into one’s eyes.

The first is Hwan-gol Tal-tae. When the body is completely remade, it becomes impossible to tell that Blood-Eye Essence was put in. This method is as difficult as mastering the Nine Transformations Demonic Art, so it is not worth considering right now.

The second method is to change not my entire body, but only my eyes.

Can eyes be changed, you ask?

They can. In this era, there is exactly one person who can change my eyes. What’s more, he is within our cult. The problem is whether he will actually grant my request.

The person in question greeted me with the heavy stench of blood.

“Second Young Master, welcome.”

“Have you been well, Elder Ma-ui?”

That person was none other than Ma-ui. The blood on his clothes belonged to a patient.

“I am always the same.”

“Please take breaks as you work. You look tired.”

“Thank you for your concern.”

I had not had much contact with Ma-ui before, but after becoming the Pavilion Master of Hwangcheongak, I had run into him several times on business and was able to build some familiarity.

“I happened to obtain some medicinal herbs, so I came to give them to you.”

I handed him the gift I had prepared. These were not ordinary herbs that came into the infirmary, but ones I had specially purchased from outside at great expense.

“Oh, such precious herbs. Thank you.”

“Not at all.”

“Come, have a cup of tea.”

“Yes.”

I drank tea with Ma-ui. After exchanging a few casual words, Ma-ui asked in an offhand manner.

“It seems you came because you have business with me?”

“That is correct. In truth, I came because I have a request to make.”

“Tell me.”

“You are a busy man, so I will not beat around the bush. Please perform the New Eye Art on me.”

For an instant, Ma-ui was greatly startled.

The New Eye Art.

It was a procedure that strengthened a martial artist’s eyes. Once one received the New Eye Art, not only ordinary vision but even dynamic visual acuity improved by leaps and bounds. In addition, one would not be easily affected by external influences entering through the eyes. Light or darkness, underwater or amid smoke—none of those external environments would affect them.

As the name implied, one was reborn with new eyes. Its effects were so extraordinary that it was also called the Divine Eye Art.

If I received this New Eye Art, even if Cult Leader Pungcheon examined my eyes, he would not realize that I had put Blood-Eye Essence into them. Unfortunately, however, the New Eye Art was known as a secret art that had been lost long ago.

Ma-ui’s expression sank completely.

“How did you know that I can perform the New Eye Art?”

Since it was not something that could come out by chance, Ma-ui did not pretend ignorance. What he wanted to know was how I knew.

I would only learn that Ma-ui had mastered the New Eye Art far in the future.

When I returned to the cult again to obtain materials for the Great Art, Ma-ui had already died. His chief disciple, Ho Baek, held the position of Divine Physician then, and I learned everything through him.

Ho Baek offered the New Eye Art to Ju Baekdo, who was the cult leader at the time.

Because of that, it came to light that Ma-ui had passed the New Eye Art and the necessary medicines to his disciple, Ho Baek.

I did not say whom I had heard it from. Rather, if I did not say it, there would be one person Ma-ui would think of.

“Ah!”

His expression turned grave. He would think that Father had told me. There was a reason he could not help but think so.

According to the recollections of his chief disciple Ho Baek, Father had refused when Ma-ui offered to perform the New Eye Art on him. Ho Baek had recalled that thanks to that, the materials for the New Eye Art were passed on to him, allowing him to perform it on the cult leader of that time, and that everything was thanks to his master.

That was why Ma-ui would grant my request.

Though Father had refused the New Eye Art, the fact that he had told me about it could be interpreted as an intention for Ma-ui to perform the New Eye Art on me.

“Do you know that one of the materials needed for the New Eye Art, the fruit of the Paradise Guanyin Herb, is so extremely difficult to obtain that the New Eye Art can be performed only once in an era?”

“Yes, I know.”

“And knowing that, you still want the New Eye Art?”

“I do.”

Ma-ui fell into thought. I gave him time to think. I waited quietly while drinking tea, and before long Ma-ui opened his mouth.

“If I perform the New Eye Art for you, Second Young Master, what will you do for me?”

“I have nothing to give you right now. Instead…”

Looking at Ma-ui, I spoke with eyes full of certainty.

“I will become the cult leader.”

“!”

“When I become the cult leader, I should be able to grant whatever it is that you wish for, Ma-ui.”

Ma-ui’s gaze wavered. I knew what Ma-ui wanted.

Ma-ui desired it until the day he died, but Father never permitted it in the end. That was also the reason Father had refused the New Eye Art. If he received the New Eye Art, he would have had to grant Ma-ui’s request.

But unlike Father, I had no intention of refusing. I intended to grant Ma-ui’s request.

“Will you grant it, no matter what it is?”

“Yes. However, only one thing.”

“One request is enough.”

“I promise.”

Now, there was one final thing left for Ma-ui to judge.

“Are you confident you can become the successor?”

“I am.”

This time, I asked.

“How do you see it, Elder? Do you think I will become the successor?”

Given my recent actions, Ma-ui must certainly have been paying attention as well.

“I believe your abilities are more than sufficient. In fact, one could say they overflow. However… you are too clear. I cannot be certain whether you truly suit the position of cult leader.”

“That works out even better.”

“What do you mean?”

“Please use the New Eye Art to hide these clear eyes. Make them into new eyes.”

I said it like a joke, but Ma-ui did not laugh. He was facing the choice of a lifetime, and he knew that choice would determine his own fate.

“This is an important decision, so please think it over and contact me.”

I rose from my seat.

“Second Young Master, let me ask one final thing.”

“Yes.”

“If you become the cult leader, how do you intend to deal with the Murim Alliance?”

“That will depend on the situation at the time. But one thing is certain. Whether orthodox or unorthodox, individual or organization, man or woman, if I judge them to be absolute evil, I will surely eliminate them.”

Knowing that, at least for him, it was the model answer, I left that place with an easy heart.

* * *

Three days later, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon visited the infirmary.

Ma-ui was sitting blankly in the treatment room, and the fatigue from several days of deliberation had settled over his face.

As he entered the room, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said,

“Is it your time to die? Why are you doing something you never do?”

Ma-ui had sent someone to say he wanted to meet in secret. That was why he had quietly come here at such a late hour.

“For us, the time to die is not far off.”

“Shut your unlucky mouth. Or stop saying ‘us.’”

“Why do you have so many lingering attachments to this world?”

“The ones who say they have no attachments to the world usually have an even worse hell inside them.”

Not many people knew that the two of them were such close friends.

“What is it? Seeing as you called me, have you finally gotten sick of saving people? Shall we go kill people together?”

Ma-ui smiled faintly. He and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who always blurted out whatever he wanted without restraint, were oddly well matched. The saying that people become close because their personalities are different made sense when one looked at his relationship with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

There had been times when he had thought he wanted to live like the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, saying everything he wanted to say.

“I called you because I wanted to ask you something.”

“What is it?”

“Why did you choose the Second Young Master?”

For a moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon stared intently at Ma-ui. Then he burst into hearty laughter.

“That bastard truly doesn’t have a place his hands can’t reach. Seeing as he’s even calling the infirmary’s prim scholar out to the battlefield. Why are you asking that?”

After a brief pause, Ma-ui answered.

“Like you said, perhaps I’ve grown tired of saving people.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon laughed heartily, but Ma-ui remained serious.

“Why the Second Young Master?”

“Why else? I chose him because he seems like he’ll become the cult leader.”

Ma-ui nodded. He was under the misunderstanding that the cult leader had told Geom Mugeuk about his New Eye Art.

Judging by that fact, there was a high possibility that the Second Young Master would become the successor.

But Ma-ui needed more than that. He stood at the crossroads of whether or not to use the one chance he had in his entire life. And on that depended whether or not he could fulfill his lifelong wish.

“So I’m asking what the reason is.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s honest answer was this:

Before I knew it, I got swept up by that bastard. When I came to my senses, I was putting the Divine Pill Beyond Heaven into his mouth.

But he could not bring himself to say that.

“The Second Young Master said this. In his demonic path, people do not smash tables at inns. That is why I chose him.”

Ma-ui stared blankly at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon for a moment. He had not expected such an absurd answer, but strangely, he felt as though he understood what that meant. Geom Mugeuk’s heart, and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s heart as well.

“Well, it is about time you stopped breaking them.”

Ma-ui rose from his seat.

“You call a man here and now you’re leaving? We should have a drink.”

“Next time. I’ll pay then.”

“What next time is there for old men? Pay today!”

But Ma-ui had already left.

Watching his back, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon let out a hollow laugh.

“With that kind of diligence, he won’t die easily.”

Of course, he was not talking about Ma-ui, but Geom Mugeuk.

* * *

I opened my eyes in the middle of sleep.

The Heavenly Demon Body Protection Art had informed me of a visitor’s presence.

I rose from my bed, belted on my sword, and went outside. Ma-ui was standing in the yard.

“Elder, you have come?”

“I released no demonic energy at all. How did you know I had come?”

“I opened my eyes while sleeping. As if it were fate.”

The reason I deliberately added that commonplace word, fate, was because it worked extremely well on older people. At their age, there were many things that were difficult to sort through unless they decided that it had all been fate.

“I have spent my entire life saving people.”

“That is a noble thing.”

Ma-ui shook his head.

“I am not a self-sacrificing person. The reason I saved people was because I wanted to kill one person.”

“What do you mean?”

I asked as if I did not know, but I already knew. That was also the reason Ma-ui had thrown himself into our cult.

“For a physician to kill someone is to defy the will of heaven. That is why. To wash away even a little of that sin, I strove to save even one more person.”

After letting out a long sigh, Ma-ui asked me,

“If you become the cult leader, will you kill that person for me?”

“Yes.”

“Why do you not ask who it is?”

“If it is someone you wish to kill, Elder, then he must surely be a wicked man. I trust you.”

“How do you know what lies inside me?”

“I believe there are times when the shell is more important than what is inside. The shell called Ma-ui is not one that can be easily obtained. I trust the effort it took to create the name Ma-ui.”

Of course, my trust in him was not based on that alone. I trusted him because I knew everything about him—his life and death, even the person he wanted to kill. If not, I would never entrust my eyes to him.

“It will not be easy to kill him. If things go wrong, many people will be swept up and die. That is why my wish is to kill only him, without any other sacrifices. Can you promise me you will do that?”

This was probably why Father had refused. To grant his request, Father would have had to move personally, and the position of Heavenly Demon was far too heavy a thing to be moved for one individual’s grudge.

“I will become stronger and kill only him without unnecessary sacrifice.”

“Will you promise me?”

“Yes.”

“Even if I die first, will you keep this promise?”

“I promise. When I drive my sword into his heart, I will whisper to him that it is the sword Ma-ui sent.”

Perhaps the mere thought pleased him, for a smile formed at Ma-ui’s lips.

At last, Ma-ui made up his mind.

“Very well. I will perform the New Eye Art on you.”

“Thank you very much.”

I bowed to him in courtesy.

“Give your thanks after the New Eye Art is finished. Now, let us go.”

“You intend to do it now?”

“Once I’ve made up my mind, I should do it right away. Who knows what my mind will be like tomorrow? Why? Do you dislike it?”

“Of course not. Let us go.”

I followed Ma-ui as he strode away.

As I did, I carefully looked around. This was the last world I would see with these eyes.

Chapter 60: There Are Many Hidden Tails
---------------------------------------
The place the Demon Physician headed for was not the infirmary.

He took me to his own residence, where a hidden chamber had been prepared underground for important procedures.

The display cabinets lining the walls were filled with all manner of medicine bottles and herbs whose contents I could not identify. Even I, an expert when it came to medicinal herbs, saw many herbs there for the first time.

“It is a dangerous procedure, so there is much to prepare.”

“Please take your time.”

“What will you do if I fail? You could lose your sight.”

I could tell he was asking to test my resolve. Failing the Divine Eye Art did not cause blindness. If it had carried that sort of risk, he would never have dared offer the procedure to the Heavenly Demon.

“If I am a patient even the Demon Physician fails to save, then it must be because my fate is unfortunate.”

At my trust, the Demon Physician gave a faint chuckle and took the materials needed for the procedure from a secret safe.

Soon after, he lit incense to calm the mind and sterilized the needles. He mixed several medicines, and some he boiled and cooled.

Meanwhile, I lay on the bed with my upper garment removed, a warm cloth scented with medicine resting over both my eyes.

“Now then, I shall begin.”

“I entrust myself to you.”

He put several medicines into my eyes at intervals. Some were as clear as water, and others as red as blood. There was a green liquid like poison, and one as blue as my sword aura. Because I trusted the Demon Physician completely, I entrusted my eyes to him with an easy heart.

After injecting the various medicines into my eyes, the Demon Physician applied the needles. He placed them densely not only around my eyes, but also across my face and chest.

“Now, sleep for a while.”

My eyes closed on their own. In my heart, I spoke to the Heavenly Demon Protective Art. Just for today, you should loosen up and sleep deeply too.

I slept well for the first time in ages.

I slept so soundly that I wondered how long it had been since I had last slept so deeply.

When I awoke, all the needles that had been placed in my face and body had already been removed.

The Demon Physician was sitting at his desk reading a book, but when I woke, he walked over to me.

“How do you feel?”

“Good.”

“And your eyes?”

“Even better.”

My eyes had truly become clear. My already excellent eyesight had improved even further, and my field of vision was unbelievably clear. It felt as though I had stepped out of a fog; to exaggerate slightly, my surroundings were so vivid that I felt as if I had been living with my eyes closed until now.

“Follow me.”

Together with him, I left the hidden chamber and went up to the first floor. We stood side by side by the window. The sun was high in the sky.

“Look at the sun.”

Since I was staring directly at the sun, it should have been dazzling, but it was not dazzling at all.

“How is it?”

“It doesn’t hurt my eyes.”

“Look far over there.”

Things at a distance I would not have been able to see before were now visible with perfect clarity. It was truly astonishing how well I could see. On top of that, when I focused my eyes, I could even see things I should not have been able to see.

“I can see clearly. I can even see the ant crawling on that tree branch over there.”

At my words, the Demon Physician laughed.

“I’m not joking.”

“I know you are not. I laughed because I was pleased.”

“Ah, this really is incredible.”

“Now, follow me again.”

This time, the Demon Physician took me to a pitch-black room prepared in the underground hidden chamber.

My vision, which previously would have been faint unless I raised my inner energy, was now clear even without doing so.

“I can see clearly. The medicine bottle over there, the painting on the wall, everything.”

“That is enough. The procedure was a perfect success. Your eyes have been reborn.”

“Thank you. Truly, thank you.”

When I first wanted the Divine Eye Art, my main purpose had been to avoid the eyes of the leader of the Pungcheon Cult. Perhaps that was why I had not expected much from its effects.

But the Divine Eye Art was far more extraordinary than I had imagined. There was only one way to describe this state.

My eyes had reached perfection.

After leaving the dark room, I bowed to the Demon Physician.

“Thank you.”

Flustered, the Demon Physician lifted me up.

“What are you doing?”

“Senior, you used on me a secret art that can only be used once in a lifetime. I will surely repay this grace by keeping my promise.”

“Thank you. When you become the cult leader, I will tell you whom you must kill.”

The Demon Physician gripped my hand firmly, and I put strength into mine as well.

When I left the Demon Physician’s residence, everything I saw around me looked different. It truly felt as though a new world had unfolded before me.

Suddenly, a thought occurred to me.

‘The Blood Eye Essence, and now the Divine Eye Art. The Pungcheon Cult and my eyes were intertwined like fate. In any case, thank you, Leader of the Pungcheon Cult.’

And so the leader of the Pungcheon Cult received a mysterious word of thanks.

* * *

I realized anew just how important improved eyesight was in martial arts.

My techniques became more precise, and as precise as they became, they became stronger.

In truth, it would be of even greater help in actual combat. I would dodge attacks I could not have avoided before, and see opportunities I could not have seen.

It would also be a great help when running with the Swift Step. I had been worried that when I eventually reached perfection, whether my eyesight would be able to keep up with such extreme speed. Now that worry was gone. I had gained the most powerful eyes a human could possess.

After leaving the work of Hwangcheon Pavilion to Seo Daeryong, I shut myself inside the training ground for a while, giving my martial arts and body time to adapt to my new eyes.

After being immersed in martial training for some time like that, I went to see Ian for the first time in a while.

“Ian, let’s play!”

Ian was absorbed in martial training.

“No! I have to practice this twenty times in a row.”

“What kind of technique practice do you do twenty times a day?”

“It’s sixty times a day. Twenty times is just the evening training.”

“Good heavens. You’ll fall ill at this rate.”

“I’m already ill. I have an illness where I can’t sleep if I don’t train.”

“Let’s rest just for today. If you go drink with me, you’ll sleep well.”

“After I finish.”

“How many times do you have left?”

“Eight. If you keep interrupting me like that, it’ll take even longer.”

I deliberately pouted and watched her practice her martial arts.

“Your waist didn’t turn enough.”

“Your left side is open.”

“Faster this time.”

As I corrected her posture, I once again felt that Ian had outstanding talent in martial arts. She understood quickly and learned well.

I watched her techniques a couple more times, then, realizing there was nothing else to tell her today, immersed myself in training the Heavenly Demon Protective Art.

The martial art I had trained the most while dealing with the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was the Heavenly Demon Protective Art. The more I practiced it, the more new it felt.

As always, it resonated with me more deeply than anything else. It felt as though I were learning a living martial art. Then again, it was precisely because it resonated with me so deeply that it had awakened me from sleep.

How many times had I repeated the training of the Heavenly Demon Protective Art like that?

When I opened my eyes, Ian was looking at me from a short distance away.

“Are you finished?”

“Yes. I completed all twenty.”

“Let’s go. Time to drink! I’m so hungry I could die.”

“Of course you are. You went three full days without eating.”

“What? Three days?”

“Yes. You circulated your qi and regulated your breathing for no less than three days.”

“I did?”

“You came to see me three days ago. You seemed to have fallen into a state of selflessness, so I deliberately didn’t speak to you.”

I was shocked. It had not felt as though I had trained for even one hour, let alone several.

“I must have been asleep.”

“I checked because I wondered if that was the case, but you weren’t sleeping. You even muttered things to yourself.”

It seemed I had truly entered a state of selflessness. I had no memory at all of what I had trained.

“Wait a moment.”

I sat down and activated the Heavenly Demon Protective Art once. My true qi moved far more smoothly and precisely than before.

As I stood up, I was smiling brightly. There had been a great advancement in the Heavenly Demon Protective Art. It seemed the training I had accumulated all this time had, through this state of selflessness, led to a rise in realm.

“You made progress, didn’t you?”

She would have known just from my expression. I nodded with a broad smile.

“Enough that I can buy you a proper meal.”

“Congratulations, young master.”

“Thank you. It’s thanks to you.”

“Why is it thanks to me?”

“If you hadn’t insisted on finishing your training, I wouldn’t have sat beside you and fallen into that state of selflessness.”

“Ah, that’s true! Buy me food!”

“I will. I’ll buy you everything you want!”

I hurriedly left the training ground with Ian.

“You starved too, didn’t you?”

“No. I ate.”

But Ian’s stomach was rumbling.

She was not the sort of person who would eat alone while I fasted through qi circulation and breathing. She had probably even stopped training for three days out of fear she might disturb my state of selflessness.

“You foolish bear.”

“No, that’s not right. If I were really a foolish bear, I wouldn’t have let you find out I starved. See, by letting you notice like this, I’m showing off just how loyal a subordinate I am. I turned it into the perfect chance to earn points.”

“You’re a fox. A fox.”

“Of course. I still have many tails hidden away.”

I laughed in good spirits.

“It’s been a long time since I walked together with you like this, young master.”

“I suppose it has.”

“It’s nice, as expected.”

That added phrase, “as expected,” truly put a person in a good mood. I like it too, Ian.

Together with Ian, I entered the Pungryu Tavern in Maga Village.

“Welcome! It has been a long time, Pavilion Master.”

Seeing Jo Chunbae greet us so warmly made me think of his cooking, and I grew even hungrier.

“Bring the food quickly!”

“Yes, I’ll run with lightness skill!”

After giving us liquor first, Jo Chunbae ran to the kitchen.

On empty stomachs, we drank a cup of liquor first.

“Khah! That’s killer.”

“Urgh! It’s strong. I really feel like I might die.”

A little while later, when the dishes came out, we devoured everything ravenously without caring about the eyes of others.

Then, patting our full bellies, we drank and talked about martial arts. She asked me everything she had usually been curious about, and I answered sincerely to the best of my knowledge.

My Bicheon Sword Art had been stagnating since the breaking of its tenth-star perfection, and I hoped that the Divine Eye Art I had undergone this time would have a good influence on it.

As we talked about martial arts, the topic naturally shifted to Seo Daeryong.

“By the way, Investigator Seo is doing well, right?”

“I suppose he is. I’ve been training lately too, so I haven’t seen him at all.”

“Everyone’s busy.”

“I don’t know for certain, but he’ll do well. Both with work and with training.”

“It’s fascinating to see Investigator Seo getting along with Elder Demon Blade.”

“I don’t know if they’re getting along. He might be getting scolded all the time and wetting his pillow every night.”

Ian burst into laughter, saying it was funny.

“I don’t think that’s the case. Elder Demon Blade seems like a subtly good person.”

“But you must not let your guard down. Just because he’s a good person to Investigator Seo doesn’t guarantee he’ll be a good person to you. Your opponent is a Demon Venerable. Be careful.”

“Yes, I’ll keep it in mind.”

“One day, you’ll have to defeat him with your own skill.”

For an instant, Ian flinched.

“Me?”

Unlike before, she did not say, “How could I possibly defeat a Demon Venerable with my skill?” Now that she had received the Bicheon Sword Art, saying something like that would be rude to me and to my father.

“I’ll do my best.”

“You know, right? Half-baked skill is the first to die. Since you’ve set foot on this path, you have to go all the way.”

“Yes!”

I raised my cup, and she drank together with me.

“And once your Bicheon Sword Art reaches a certain level, let’s properly start gathering subordinates for the Ghost Shadow Squad. If we gather the squad leaders properly, the rest will be easy.”

“Do you already have people in mind?”

I nodded honestly.

“One or two, yes. Later, you’ll go with me to recruit them.”

“Who are they?”

“If you find out now, you’ll only be startled for no reason. I’ll tell you when the time comes.”

“Now that you put it that way, my heart is already pounding.”

“For times like that, there’s a fine solution called liquor, isn’t there?”

The sound of our cups clinking was as cheerful as our mood.

“Let’s order more liquor and more side dishes too.”

“Unfortunately, I think we’ll have to stop drinking for today.”

Following my gaze, Ian looked down at the first floor.

Seo Daeryong, who had hurried into the tavern, came up to our seats on the second floor and reported on the next one I would have to cook.

“The leader of the Pungcheon Cult has just arrived at our main cult.”

Chapter 61: Will you kill me too?
---------------------------------
The Pungcheon Cult Leader and the Demon Buddha entered the Heavenly Demon Hall.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader, Ling Paisu, had a commanding presence and a healthy complexion, with an intense gaze that seemed capable of boring through walls. He was a man who had mastered the Calamitous Heaven Great Demonic Qi, known as the foremost art beyond the border, and he was the absolute ruler who governed the martial world of the Outer Territories.

There had once been a time when the Blood Cult stood shoulder to shoulder with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, but now that it had become the Pungcheon Cult, its power had weakened considerably by comparison and could no longer be set against the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

This time, when he left the Pungcheon Cult, he brought with him the Ten Great Demons, the ten supreme masters who represented the cult, as well as one hundred elite soldiers of the Blood Na Army.

And that was not all. He had brought every sacred relic that had been under his authority, including the Yin Thunder Bell and the Blood Buddha. One might think it safer simply to lock them away inside a prison of ten-thousand-year cold iron and have the experts he had brought guard them, but he absolutely refused to be separated from the sacred relics.

The one who had guaranteed his safety within the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult was the Demon Buddha, Chu Luoban, who walked in beside him. He was a man of short stature, only about half the height of an ordinary adult.

But what made anyone in the world turn their head to look at him was not his height. It was the color of his skin.

His face and hands, and indeed his entire body, were golden. Thus he gave the impression of a small golden Buddha statue moving about like a child monk, and it was known to be because of the Golden Great Luo Demonic Art he had cultivated.

The two walked side by side along the Path of Blood and arrived below the grand chair.

First, the Pungcheon Cult Leader bowed respectfully in greeting.

“I greet the master of the Divine Cult and the sovereign of the martial world under heaven. Thank you for inviting me here.”

The Heavenly Demon, Geom Ujin, greeted him with a smile.

“Welcome. How many years has it been?”

It was a calm greeting, yet every word flew into the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s ears as though lodging there. Though no internal energy was imbued in it, the fact that it struck more powerfully than words that did carry internal energy was a clear indication of how lofty the Heavenly Demon’s realm was.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader was confident before anyone in the world, yet before the Heavenly Demon alone, he felt cowed.

“It seems it has been nearly ten years.”

“Have you been well?”

“Thanks to your concern, I have been without trouble.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader had resolved that if he met the Heavenly Demon, he absolutely must not be pushed back in the contest of wills. But at this moment, a thought came to him. A contest of wills—why bother with something so useless?

This time, Geom Ujin’s gaze turned toward the Demon Buddha.

“You’ve worked hard making such a long journey.”

“It was a pleasant journey to visit a friend.”

Just as the Demon Buddha was about to add something, showing off his friendship with the Pungcheon Cult Leader, Geom Ujin cut him off flatly.

“It is late, and you must be tired from coming all this way, so please go and rest for today. Let us meet again when it is light.”

“Yes. Then I shall withdraw.”

The greetings ended formally and briefly.

Originally, the Heavenly Demon and the Pungcheon Cult Leader were not close. That was because the Pungcheon Cult had, for generations, been close not to the Heavenly Demon Hall but to the Eight Demon Venerables.

It was only natural. Historically, there had been several wars between the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Blood Cult. Since they were not born to be friendly with each other, it was only expected that the Eight Demon Venerables, who stood opposed to the Heavenly Demon Hall, would join hands with the Pungcheon Cult.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader walked out of the Heavenly Demon Hall together with the Demon Buddha.

“Still, the supreme ruler of the Outer Territories has come all this way. The least he could have done was offer a cup of tea. How excessive.”

“It is late at night.”

“Then he should have treated you even better.”

At the Demon Buddha’s complaint, the Pungcheon Cult Leader spoke as though it were nothing.

“Even if you do not try to drive a wedge between us like that, I have no intention of becoming close with your cult leader. So stop.”

“Drive a wedge? This concerns your pride and honor.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader understood why the Heavenly Demon had cut off the Demon Buddha’s words. The Demon Buddha was good at saying things that touched people’s emotions from time to time. He had a nasty habit of saying things there was no need to say, turning the other person’s insides upside down.

“Let us go see the corpse first.”

“You will not see it tomorrow? You must be tired. Sleep first, and let us see it tomorrow.”

“We can sleep when we are dead.”

“Then let us do that. Why is everyone so stubborn?”

The Demon Buddha led him to the place where the corpse was kept. The body of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had been stored in a place as cold as an icehouse, with medicine applied to it so it would not decay.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader immediately examined the corpse.

The Demon Buddha watched from the side, his face filled with curiosity.

With practiced movements, the Pungcheon Cult Leader thoroughly inspected the inside of the corpse. He touched each of the organs and meridians with his hands, sniffed them, and even injected inner force into the cold corpse.

At last, after quite a long examination, he finished.

“The eight extraordinary meridians were damaged, and a great strain was placed on the heart. This happened while he was continuously unleashing soul-capturing arts.”

“Are you saying it was qi deviation? Or are you saying it was not?”

“The cause of death was indeed qi deviation. But it is certain that the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable died while fighting someone in the world he had opened.”

The Demon Buddha’s expression hardened.

“So he was indeed killed by someone’s hand.”

“I am certain. The problem is that he died despite using soul-capturing arts.”

The Demon Buddha shook his head.

“It is hard to believe. Who could be skilled enough to infiltrate Seohwanjin, kill Soul-Capturing, and escape?”

At that, the Pungcheon Cult Leader pointed to the sky with his finger. It was a question: had the Heavenly Demon killed him?

The Demon Buddha shook his head.

“We determined that was not the case. Soul-Capturing was one of our greatest assets when dealing with those orthodox faction bastards. The Cult Leader did not dislike Soul-Capturing in ordinary times either. There is no way the Cult Leader killed him.”

Compared to the cautious Pungcheon Cult Leader, the Demon Buddha mentioned the Cult Leader comfortably. It was because he was certain it was not him.

“Then is there someone else considered the culprit?”

“There is one person.”

“Who is it?”

“The Cult Leader’s second son.”

In that instant, the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s expression hardened. He had not imagined that they would suspect the Cult Leader’s son as the culprit.

But when he thought about it from another angle, he realized that precisely because it was the Cult Leader’s son, he might possess the ability to do such a thing.

He had not said a word about this the entire way here. Though anger boiled inside him at having been dragged into such a dangerous situation, the Pungcheon Cult Leader showed no change in emotion whatsoever.

For if someone tripped him, he was the kind of man who would pick up even a stone as he rose, and he was also the kind of man who would keep smiling until he smashed that stone into their head.

“Tomorrow, let us see the Second Young Master first.”

* * *

Seo Daeryong finished his morning report and was about to turn away when he spoke to me.

“Are you all right?”

“What do you mean?”

“Now even the Outer Territories are coming after you, Pavilion Master.”

“Tell them all to come at me.”

“I will. As long as I’m not there when that fight happens.”

“Sorry, but you’ll always be there. You’ll be called my right arm, after all.”

Seo Daeryong deliberately clutched his head and pretended to be distressed.

“You said you would grow gloomy if you learned martial arts, but you are still bright.”

“That is… I thought I would grow gloomy too, but to be honest, learning martial arts is enjoyable.”

“Are you sure it is not that you like the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, rather than liking martial arts?”

After a brief pause, Seo Daeryong said,

“…I do not dislike him. For now.”

“For now? That sounds like a warning that you will like him more and more.”

“If that happens… I think you will have to cut off your right arm and find one gloomy person to replace me.”

“Where would I find someone as gloomy, crooked, and amusing as you? Don’t even dream of running away from me.”

“I like that just fine, though.”

Just then, a subordinate of the Hwangcheon Pavilion reported.

“The Pungcheon Cult Leader has arrived.”

“Show him in.”

Seo Daeryong quickly left the office and said,

“Do not lose!”

“Stay with me.”

“In life, every man must fend for himself!”

“I’ve heard that somewhere before! Are you already starting to resemble me?”

Seo Daeryong left without even looking back.

A moment later, the Pungcheon Cult Leader entered.

“I greet the supreme ruler of the Outer Territories.”

When I greeted him respectfully, the Pungcheon Cult Leader smiled.

“Second Young Master, it has been a long time. I saw you when you were young, but now you have become an adult.”

“You remain as vigorous as ever, Cult Leader.”

Though he was smiling with a kindly face, the Pungcheon Cult Leader had been probing me from the moment he entered.

“I heard that you had arrived. Your schedule must be busy, so may I ask why you have come to see me?”

“Even from far away, I have ears that hear. Recently, word of the Second Young Master’s fame has reached our cult.”

“It is nothing but an undeserved reputation.”

“And humble as well.”

After exchanging such formal greetings, we sat facing one another.

“The Cult Leader seemed greatly saddened by the death of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.”

He lightly mentioned my father as he brought up the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable. He would be trying to discern something from every answer I gave.

“He was one of the Demon Venerables my father cherished most.”

“Who could have killed him?”

“Was he murdered? I heard he died after falling into qi deviation.”

At that very moment, a strand of energy flowed from the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s eyes. Normally, it would have been a hidden energy that left no trace, but to me, that energy was visible.

In that instant, I realized I had gained a special ability.

Formless energy was visible to my eyes. It was energy I would normally have only sensed, but now I could see it like a heat haze.

It was not only because of the Divine Eye Art, nor only because of the Blood-Eye Essence. As these two combined, a new ability had been born in me. Whether it worked only on Blood Cult martial arts or whether it would work on the energy of other martial artists as well, I did not yet know.

“Why are you smiling?”

“It feels good to see you after so long.”

“I feel good as well.”

Even in the midst of this, his energy examined every corner of my body. After inspecting every part of me, it finally examined my eyes. He was checking whether I had perhaps placed some divine substance like the Blood-Eye Essence into them.

But it was impossible for him to discover anything in my eyes, which had newly changed through the Divine Eye Art.

The energy he had released was drawn back into his eyes. Not feeling it, but seeing it, was truly an astonishing thing.

“I shall be going now.”

“What a shame. Please stay a little longer.”

“I have seen your face, so that is enough. I should not keep a busy man.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader rose from his seat.

“I will see you again next time. Thank you for visiting.”

Thank you for the ability I gained thanks to you.

From before my regression until now, the Pungcheon Cult Leader had been giving generously to me. Of course, he was not giving because he wanted to, but in any case, fate kept making me receive things from him. That was why I could not entirely dislike him.

* * *

The Demon Buddha was waiting for the Pungcheon Cult Leader as he left Geom Mugeuk’s office.

“How was it?”

“The Second Young Master had absolutely nothing to do with soul-capturing arts. To subdue him inside the space Soul-Capturing created, one would have to cultivate Buddhist or Daoist martial arts to the extreme, or cultivate sinister arts of a similar level, but there were no traces of that whatsoever.”

“Are you saying Geom Mugeuk is not the culprit?”

“That is correct.”

“Are you certain?”

“If you do not trust me, why did you call me all the way to this distant place?”

“It is an important matter, so I was confirming it again. Do not take offense.”

Though he said that, the Demon Buddha did not seem to believe him completely.

“I am certain.”

“Let us go. I will summon the Seven Demon Venerables, so please speak before them.”

“No. You go and convey my words. It is the same statement either way.”

“Will that be all right?”

“What would not be all right about it?”

The Demon Buddha knew that the Pungcheon Cult Leader had many complaints about this trip to the Central Plains. So he did not press him further.

“Then let us do that.”

The Demon Buddha bid him farewell and left.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s gaze as he watched his retreating back was not warm.

There was another reason he had not gone to the meeting of the Seven Demon Venerables. It was because he would have had to lie not before one Demon Buddha, but before seven people.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader looked back at the residence where Geom Mugeuk was and murmured quietly.

“Second Young Master, just how did you kill the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable?”
