“Things neglected as trivial inevitably become the greatest problems. This incident is clearly the Demon Lords’ mistake.”

I could glimpse Father’s anguish. In his heart of hearts, he probably wanted to pit the Demon Lords against the Pungcheon Cult Leader and watch from the sidelines, then eliminate those lacking loyalty from among them.

But if they, the main force of our cult, were to collapse, it was obvious that the Murim Alliance would come charging in. Thus, the Eight Demon Lords were truly a necessary evil to Father.

“Then why did the Pungcheon Cult Leader come to me instead of paying his respects to you, Father?”

Father was firm in his answer this time as well.

“The first reason is that he finds me difficult. The second is that he considers you easy to use.”

“What would he try to do by using me?”

I asked, feigning ignorance.

“Ask your strategist.”

“I don’t have a strategist.”

“Get one.”

“Where would I find someone as astute as Strategist Sima?”

Father merely snorted.

“The leisure of one who already has!”

Finding a strategist as brilliant as Sima Ming was truly my top priority.

“Aren’t you doing well even without one?”

“Thank you for saying so, but most of these matters resolved themselves thanks to good fortune.”

I was navigating situations based on experience from before my regression; I was not a strategist-type myself. I desperately needed a strategist with foresight and insight to assist me.

“Please answer just this one question. What does the Pungcheon Cult Leader want?”

Father stopped walking and answered.

“His dream is to advance into the Central Plains.”

“But he is someone who refuses to leave his throne even to protect a sacred relic.”

“That is his obsessive nature.”

“So his dream is to advance into the Central Plains.”

In my previous life before the regression, the Pungcheon Cult Leader had failed to advance into the Central Plains. Because Hwamugi swept through the whole of Murim, he could not set a single foot there and ended his life beyond the frontier.

But because I regressed, his life was changing as well. He had come to the Central Plains, and even directly to our Main Cult, a place he would never have come to otherwise.

“For generations, the Blood Cult beyond the frontier has targeted the Central Plains. Though they have never succeeded even once, the desire to advance into the Central Plains runs in their blood.”

“Now I understand why he came to me.”

It was something I already knew without asking Father, but I acted as though I had learned it from him. Sometimes you must act ignorant even when you are not. Rather than being repulsive, it was courtesy toward Father.

Most parents in the world would wish for their child to come and ask them such things. Would my father be any different?

At least in this regard, he was no different. If he were, he would not have spoken these words.

As he was about to go inside, Father added curtly:

“Since you’re already here, eat before you go.”

“Yes!”

There was nothing I liked more than Father telling me to stay for a meal.

But I did not say I was pleased. If I had, he would never tell me to eat before leaving again.

An absolutely difficult father.

* * *

After eating with Father and returning to the Hwangcheon Pavilion, I received a visit from someone completely unexpected. It was Mabul, come to find me.

“Welcome, elder.”

“I said I would come once, but I am late.”

It was the first time I had seen him this close. He was shorter than he appeared from afar, and looking at his golden skin, I felt an urge to touch it with my hand.

Mabul.

He was truly my older brother’s right hand.

“Here, have a seat.”

“Very well.”

Mabul sat down. He hated being looked down upon by anyone. Even seated, he held his back perfectly straight for that very reason.

“My dear friend had an important sacred relic stolen. How could I remain idle? I came to see how the investigation is progressing.”

“I am personally handling this matter at present.”

“Is it true that a thief truly broke in?”

“The truth? Whatever do you mean?”

When I asked innocently, he looked at me as if wondering whether I was truly unaware.

“How can I believe that a thief broke into the inner court of our Main Cult?”

“Would it not be harder to believe that the Pungcheon Cult Leader staged a self-directed scheme?”

From the very start, we created a tense atmosphere.

A faint golden light swirled in his pupils as well. It was a phenomenon said to appear only in those who had achieved great completion in the Golden Daera Demon Art.

Just how powerful was Mabul? The Golden Daera Demon Art he had mastered was a martial art as troublesome to face as the sorceries of the Soul-Devouring Demon Art.

“If a thief truly entered the inner court, our Main Cult’s reputation will be greatly damaged.”

“That is likely.”

“Should it not be handled quietly?”

“What do you mean?”

“Why not send him back beyond the frontier for now, so that he cannot spread rumors? It is not as though his presence will enable us to catch a thief we could not otherwise catch.”

It was clearly a test. He was convinced it was a self-directed scheme, and he believed I was aiding it.

“Would he listen to the likes of a mere pavilion lord?”

“I heard the Pungcheon Cult Leader came to see you.”

“He came to pay his respects.”

“The Pungcheon Cult Leader believes you will become the successor.”

“He showed no such sign. Why do you think so?”

“Because he is not a man who seeks others out unless that is the case. He is accustomed to receiving greetings from others. If such a man sought you, he must have seen the potential for you to be the successor.”

Even as he spoke, he constantly studied my reactions, trying to read my thoughts. Unfortunately for him, that was an attempt that even my father failed at.

“Second Young Master, for the sake of the Main Cult, you must send him back.”

It was a remark aiming to kill two birds with one stone.

If I moved to send him back, that would suit him fine; if I refused the proposal, he could accuse me of failing to act for the Main Cult.

“No. In that case, we must not send him back.”

“What do you mean?”

“As you said, he will use this incident to tarnish the Main Cult’s reputation. Then we must keep him here. Rather, as long as he remains in the Main Cult, he cannot speak carelessly. I will subtly caution him. To keep his mouth shut if he wishes to recover the sacred relic.”

For a moment, Mabul was at a loss for words. Of course, he was not such a pushover that he would come to cause trouble only to receive more than he bargained for.

“The Pungcheon Cult Leader is a cunning snake. He certainly has other intentions.”

“Then I will pretend to join hands with him and uncover his true intentions.”

“Since you are so stubborn, I expect you will become an excellent Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord.”

“I am mainly so in matters I believe to be right.”

Mabul took those words as a rebuke that he himself was not a righteous man.

Judging it an opportunity, Mabul quickly formed a hand seal with his fingers.

Revolving Dharma Light Seal!

In an instant, golden radiance erupted from Mabul’s body.

In light more intense than the sun, an opponent cannot open their eyes for a moment. This fleeting instant is an eternity to those of the Demon Lord class. Should I stab here to kill, or there to kill? No—let me stab both.

I closed my eyes as well.

But before I closed them, I knew. My eyes were barely affected by that light. Golden light poured from his body, and Mabul’s figure moving at its center was clearly visible.

The one that emitted light at this moment was not the Golden Daera Demon Art, but my Divine Eye Technique.

However, I deliberately closed my eyes. There was no need to reveal my full strength to Mabul.

When I opened my eyes again, Mabul had thrust his face right before my nose.

His face, both childlike and adult-like, felt strangely unfamiliar.

The gaze with which he looked at me said exactly this:

*If I had decided to kill you, you would already be dead!*

He was someone with every right to be confident. There are only five hand seals in the Golden Daera Demon Art. How terrifying the follow-up attack would be after blinding the opponent and closing in was obvious even without experiencing it.

I said nothing. Even if not to the point of terror, I deliberately showed a startled and fearful reaction.

Perhaps deciding this was sufficient warning, Mabul rose from his seat. He said the opposite of what Hyeolcheon Doma had said when he first came to see me.

“I would like to see you often. Drinking tea like this, sharing stories. If you stand with me, only good things will come your way.”

“Please share much with my elder brother.”

At my childish arrogance, Mabul smiled in satisfaction.

“Yes, you are at an age for such things. Then let us meet again.”

“Take care.”

As he walked out of the room with brisk steps, the stiff expression I had deliberately maintained relaxed, and the room that had been dyed golden returned to its original state.

Listen here, Mabul. You had best not see me too often. The Demon Lords entangled with me still fall into only three categories.

Those who have grown close, those who are growing close, or those who are dead.

Which side do you think you are on? For now, it does not seem as though you and I will grow close….

Then do you have the confidence to create a fourth?

Chapter 68: Second Young Master to the End.
-------------------------------------------
“The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had five disciples in all, but among them, judging by age and ability, the strongest candidates in the succession struggle are the first disciple, Liang Dao, and the third disciple, Cheongseon. For reference, Cheongseon is a woman.”

At Seo Daeryong’s report, I asked,

“What is the reputation of the two of them within Seohwanjin?”

“Given his age, Liang Dao has the edge in experience and martial skill, but Cheongseon is more popular within Seohwanjin. Compared to her, young and beautiful as she is, Liang Dao has a violent temperament, and several ghost-art practitioners have already been injured by him. Some have even died. Even so, the late Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable favored him, so he had a high chance of becoming the successor, but now the situation has changed.”

“Investigate the two of them thoroughly. Their martial arts, wealth, abilities, hobbies, and personal relationships. Don’t leave out a single thing.”

“Understood.”

“I wonder whom Cult Leader Pungcheon will choose.”

At my question, Seo Daeryong’s eyes went round.

“Cult Leader Pungcheon is going to choose one of them?”

“Yes. That’s why I sent him the information on the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s disciples.”

“Why?”

“Because I told him to jump into Seohwanjin’s succession struggle.”

“Good heavens! What are you plotting this time? Don’t tell me?”

Seo Daeryong, as if struck by a thought, sent a voice transmission.

—Are you going to kill Cult Leader Pungcheon too? Or Liang Dao? Even Cheongseon?

—Am I some kind of murderer? Why would I kill them all?

Only then did Seo Daeryong look relieved.

“That’s a relief.”

“So this is what I’ve become these days. Everyone worries about whom I might kill.”

“Who else was worried? Your master?”

“My master?”

“Ah! Elder Blood-Heaven Blade Demon.”

“You still don’t call him Master, do you?”

“No, he hasn’t permitted it.”

And yet it seemed Seo Daeryong already regarded him as his master. Seeing how the word slipped out without him realizing it.

I could feel Seo Daeryong falling deeper and deeper for the Blade Demon. Well, the moment he saw the Blood-Heaven Blade Demon reading a book, Seo Daeryong had probably already plunged in headfirst.

“So whom will Cult Leader Pungcheon choose?”

“How would I know?”

“If even you, a genius who entered at the top of the class, don’t know, then how would anyone?”

“I entered at the top because I studied hard. I’m not a genius.”

“You said you were a genius, didn’t you?”

“That was what you said, Pavilion Master.”

“You nodded back then, acting all smug.”

“I admit it. I admit that there is a vanity within me that even I can’t help. So please lower my assessment from genius to a fellow merely competent at practical affairs. In exchange, I will investigate the two of them thoroughly.”

Seo Daeryong bowed and left the office.

* * *

“So, whom should we support?”

At Cult Leader Pungcheon’s question, the shackled man only stared at the Yin Thunder Bell.

“Why are you staring at that bell like that? Does the evil spirit carved into it speak to you or something?”

At that, the shackled man suddenly said,

“How did you know?”

“Why are you making this frightening?”

“It speaks to me from time to time.”

“What does it say?”

Staring blankly at the evil spirit, the shackled man said,

“Why do you live like that?”

A brief silence flowed between them.

“So what did you answer?”

“In the past, I used to answer that I lived like this because of one narrow-minded, greedy human being.”

“And now?”

“I answered that perhaps… it might not be entirely because of that human being.”

“What did it say to that?”

The shackled man said nothing. When urged once more, the shackled man looked at Cult Leader Pungcheon as if he were pathetic.

“Why do you keep asking? What would a carving say? Are you insane?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon wore an expression of disbelief.

“Of course. It would be strange if I weren’t insane. Before long, I’ll be sitting there, having a conversation with that carved evil spirit. ‘Why do you live being looked down on like that?’ Why do you think? You evil-spirit bastard, you’ve been watching all along, so you should know!”

“Cheongseon.”

“You startled me! Please, at least take a stance before you stab me!”

The shackled man was impossible to predict, but because he gave answers like this when needed, he was precious.

“Why Cheongseon? From what I see, Liang Dao seems more likely to become the successor.”

“Then choose that person.”

The shackled man closed his mouth as if he had nothing more to say.

Cult Leader Pungcheon sidled over beside him.

“I’m asking why Cheongseon.”

“Just choose Liang Dao according to your own convictions. How long will a man who is even a cult leader keep following someone else’s words?”

“Very well. According to my convictions, I choose Cheongseon.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon trusted the shackled man absolutely. He needed no reason. There was the past he had shown him until now.

“Cheongseon, you say? Then how can I take her as my disciple?”

“That, I do not know.”

“What do I do if you don’t know?”

“Would it not be stranger if I knew even that while being tied up here like this?”

“True. That is true.”

Even so, Cult Leader Pungcheon did not say he would release his shackles. Though they had exchanged all manner of words until now, he had never said those words.

If he released the shackles, the man would fly away from him. Who in the world would remain out of fondness for someone who had made him like this? The moment he freed him, it was over. That was why speaking of removing the shackles was Cult Leader Pungcheon’s absolute taboo.

“Should I go find Cheongseon?”

“What will you say when you go?”

“Become my disciple. Is that too overbearing? I shall permit you to become my disciple. How is that?”

“And she will become your disciple so very readily.”

“Then what am I supposed to do?”

“Put yourself in her position. An inscrutable old man from beyond the borders suddenly comes and tells you to become his disciple. If you were in her place, Cult Leader, would you fall for that?”

“Not a chance.”

“Others are the same. It cannot be done for nothing.”

“Don’t tell me you mean I have to pay a price?”

The shackled man nodded.

“I’m going to become her master and teach her martial arts, aren’t I? If a price is to be paid, it should be paid by the other side.”

“That is an outdated way of thinking. The price is paid by the one who is more desperate.”

“I refuse. Now I want to receive, not give. I am, by nature, a person who likes receiving far more! Tell them to come learn after bringing heaps of things to fill my collection of divine relics! Tell them to kneel in the courtyard all day and beg!”

The shackled man had told him to give everything, but that was not as easy as it sounded.

“If you must pay a price, the target should not be Cheongseon.”

“Then who?”

“The Second Young Master will want more of the divine relics here. When he looked around the relics last time, there were some he was eyeing.”

“Damn it!”

“The Second Young Master will bring the answer this time as well.”

“The Second Young Master again?”

To the sighing Cult Leader Pungcheon, the shackled man said calmly,

“It will be the Second Young Master until the very end.”

* * *

Just as Seo Daeryong had boasted, he was at least undeniably capable when it came to practical affairs.

He had investigated Liang Dao and Cheongseon in exhaustive detail. Not only their families and households, and not only their level of martial arts, but their personalities, interpersonal relationships, and even the foods they liked.

“However, in the course of investigating Cheongseon, I discovered one strange point.”

“What kind of point?”

“Cheongseon has met Sa Ujong several times.”

“Sa Ujong?”

An unexpected name had surfaced.

“Yes. Since there does not seem to be any connection between them, I am reporting it just in case.”

Because Seo Daeryong knew that I had also formed ties with the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, he was reporting it separately.

I recalled Sa Ujong’s end.

He had been caught under a soul-capturing art and tried to kill the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, only to be killed in return. It was a death in which whose soul-capturing art had been used was never ultimately revealed.

‘Ah! So that was Cheongseon’s soul-capturing art!’

A single conclusion was naturally drawn.

“The two of them were lovers!”

At my words, Seo Daeryong was startled. Not because the two had been lovers, but because I had guessed the truth on the spot.

“How did you know?”

Sa Ujong had been in love with the Ilhwa Sword Venerable. If Cheongseon had discovered that while they were together? His death was surely the result of Cheongseon’s jealousy and rage. Especially since Cheongseon was young. If her man loved an older woman, the wound to her pride would be deep enough to pierce through her body.

That was not the only reason I judged that they had been lovers.

The person who had earlier revealed the place where the corpses from the Mind-Soul Great Art had been buried, and the person who had stolen Neunghyu’s fan and placed it there, was surely Cheongseon. These were things that could not be discovered or accomplished unless one was the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s disciple. Moreover, if she had not been in a relationship with Sa Ujong, there was no way she would have leaked such important information.

All the pieces fit together, but I could not tell Seo Daeryong these facts.

I sprang up from my seat.

“You’ve thought of something that will change the situation again, haven’t you?”

“How did you know?”

“Your eyes changed. Pavilion Master, why do you look for a genius? You yourself are a genius.”

“I’m not a genius. I’m only the sort to use petty tricks and not let profitable things slip by. Not this kind of mind. I need someone who can think on a grand scale. There’s a big difference between an organization having such a person and not having one.”

“I don’t really understand, but in any case, you are remarkable.”

“What do you mean you don’t understand? I figured this out thanks to your investigation. Even if I find a genius, I won’t trade that person for you.”

“Of course you won’t. You’ll have both anyway. But where are you going?”

I was already walking out of the office.

‘To earn a divine relic!’

This, too, was something I could not tell him.

* * *

I went straight to meet Cult Leader Pungcheon.

His residence was the scene of the incident, and as the Master of Hwangcheon Pavilion, I had justification to come and go officially at any time.

“Welcome.”

“Have you been well?”

“I looked over the materials you sent. Thank you.”

“Not at all.”

As we exchanged greetings, I glanced at the shackled man.

He kept his head lowered and did not look at me, but whenever I saw him, I felt that this was not a connection I would simply brush past.

My gaze returned to Cult Leader Pungcheon.

“Have you found a way to jump into Seohwanjin’s succession struggle?”

“Not yet.”

“You must hurry. Soon the Demon Venerables will intervene and choose a successor.”

“As you know, here I wear shackles just like that man. Even meeting Seohwanjin’s successor candidates is not easy.”

I nodded as if I understood.

“Whom among the candidates would you like me to take as my disciple?”

“Cheongseon.”

Seeing that he was not surprised by my answer, it seemed he had already reached the same conclusion with the shackled man. Indeed, that was the case.

“We also judged Cheongseon to be suitable.”

For me, it was because of Sa Ujong, but for what reason had that shackled man chosen Cheongseon?

“But I have no way to persuade her.”

“What if I can make Cheongseon come directly to you, Cult Leader?”

“And after she comes?”

“She will ask to become your disciple. I will cook the rice and spoon-feed it to you.”

“If you can do that, I could ask for nothing more.”

“In exchange, give me one divine relic.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon’s expression, which had brightened, darkened again.

“You truly are shameless.”

“It is not shamelessness. It is only natural. Ordering someone to work by putting friendship or loyalty first without proper compensation was something that only worked in my father’s era.”

“I, too, am a man of that era.”

“You must acknowledge that times have changed. And that I, sitting before you, am a man of this era. If you dislike that, then you must harden like a fossil and live looking only at what pleases you.”

Of course, because Cult Leader Pungcheon did not want such a life, his expression stiffened.

I persuaded him in a gentle tone.

“This matter involves dealing with young people, so please leave it to me. I will produce a result that makes the divine relic worth it.”

“Let me think for a moment.”

“Of course.”

Pretending not to notice, I looked around at the divine relics, giving the shackled man and Cult Leader Pungcheon time to exchange voice transmissions.

At length, Cult Leader Pungcheon said to me,

“Fine. I accept your proposal.”

Though his face was full of displeasure, in the end, it seemed he had been persuaded by the shackled man.

“My role goes only as far as having her come to you and ask you to accept her as your disciple. Raising her to the position of Demon Venerable will be your ability, Cult Leader.”

“If I need help then as well, you will come again and demand another divine relic. Why not just take them all now!”

“I wish I were capable of that. For my sake, and for yours as well.”

I rose from my seat. To Cult Leader Pungcheon I was courteous, and to the shackled man I gave a fleeting nod with my eyes before leaving.

From there, I went to find one person.

The person holding the key to this matter—Sa Ujong.

Chapter 69: A Dirty Trick Is Effective.
---------------------------------------
I entered the Northern Heaven Sword Clan.

As always, Sa Ujong guided me to the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“Welcome, Pavilion Master.”

“Master Sa, have you been well?”

“Thanks to your concern, I have been well.”

He greeted me with his usual expression. Although he had failed in his attempt to use the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable to drive me away, he did not reveal his resentment.

Today, I intended to use Cheongseon as bait, luring him with his hidden ambitions.

“Welcome!”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable greeted me warmly. She seemed somehow clearer and brighter than when I had last seen her.

“You look younger than ever.”

At my greeting, she led me toward the flower bed.

“Come here and look at this.”

There were sprouts emerging from the soil where she pointed.

“It’s the seed you gave me as a gift.”

“Oh! It’s already grown.”

“Yes. Life is truly mysterious.”

I felt the energy of Sa Ujong, standing behind us, sink even lower. Ever since I had entered this place, I had been secretly releasing my qi to observe him.

My mastery had improved to a degree incomparable to when I had first learned this technique from Father. I could now discern not only another person’s presence and appearance, but even their emotions.

I secretly sent a transmission to the Sword Venerable.

―Could you have Sa Ujong bring us some wine and side dishes? I need to let him overhear some information. I’ll explain why later.

―Understood.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable had Sa Ujong bring wine. His emotions grew even more ferocious as he answered politely and turned away.

She did not usually drink, yet she was drinking now. She also spoke informally only to me.

Judging by the changes in his emotions, he seemed to think that I had taken the Ilhwa Sword Venerable away from him.

I suspected that his relationship with Cheongseon was not a normal one either. His end had told me everything.

When Sa Ujong returned with the wine and side dishes a short while later, I let one piece of information slip. I addressed the Sword Venerable, but in truth, I intended for Sa Ujong to hear it.

“At present, Yang Do, the first disciple, and Cheongseon, the third disciple, are the leading candidates to become the next Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable.”

I sensed Sa Ujong flinch at the mention of Cheongseon.

“I heard Yang Do was the stronger candidate.”

“That is true. However, it is not as if Cheongseon has no way forward.”

“What way is that?”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable continued the conversation with me. She was probably wondering why we were having such a conversation in Sa Ujong’s presence, but she showed no sign of it and acted naturally.

“Isn’t the Pungcheon Cult Leader currently visiting our church? The martial arts he and the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable learned share the same roots. Therefore, if Cheongseon could receive instruction from the Pungcheon Cult Leader, she would still have a chance.”

“Would the Pungcheon Cult Leader really accept her?”

“He will. As everyone knows, the Pungcheon Cult has long wished to advance into the Central Plains by any means necessary. If he takes Cheongseon as his disciple under these circumstances, he will gain an excuse to remain in the Central Plains. In fact, Cheongseon would be the one unwilling to accept.”

“Why is that?”

“It seems the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable was very thorough in educating her disciples. She probably never had anything good to say about her old enemy, the Pungcheon Cult Leader.”

“I suppose not.”

“If things continue as they are, Yang Do will ultimately take the position of Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable. Unfortunately, Cheongseon will lose her chance to become a Demon Venerable.”

That was all Sa Ujong heard before leaving the wine and side dishes behind. I spoke quickly, while he placed the wine and side dishes on the table slowly. He took his time walking away, listening to as much of the conversation as possible before departing.

It was enough. I had conveyed everything important that he needed to hear.

I believed Sa Ujong would certainly persuade Cheongseon to become the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s disciple. A man as ambitious as him would never let his beloved miss the opportunity to become the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable.

Once I confirmed that he had completely left, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable finally asked,

“Why did you ask me to do that?”

“Sa Ujong is in a relationship with Cheongseon. Did you know?”

“Sa Ujong? I had no idea.”

“You may or may not know this, but according to our Pavilion’s investigation, Sa Ujong is an extremely ambitious man. He is the kind of person who might try to manipulate you from behind the scenes.”

“I was vaguely aware of that.”

“While keeping an eye on him in case he caused you harm, I discovered that the two of them were involved. An opportunity arose to make use of that fact, which is why I asked this of you.”

“I see. Thank you for telling me.”

“I’m the one who should be grateful. Please remain wary of him in your heart, but for the time being, I would appreciate it if you continued handling matters involving Sa Ujong as usual.”

“I will.”

I intended to repay her for the help she had given me with what she desired most.

“Let’s have a sparring match sometime soon.”

“I look forward to it.”

To be honest, I was the one looking forward to it. I wanted to see how much the Divine Eye Technique would help me in actual combat.

But that would come after I dealt with this matter first.

As I left, I turned back. I saw her standing in the courtyard with her hands clasped behind her back, gazing up at the sky.

The little hut no longer seemed hypocritical to me.

* * *

For some reason, Sa Ujong was doing his utmost in bed tonight.

Thanks to that, Cheongseon experienced pleasure she had never felt before.

The two of them lay side by side on the bed, drenched in sweat.

“Is something going on today?”

“No. Why?”

“You’re not like usual.”

“And?”

“It felt so good.”

Sa Ujong was certainly different from usual. He embraced her first and asked,

“You’ve been busy lately, haven’t you?”

“There are people saying that I should become a Demon Venerable.”

“What do you think?”

“Who would dislike becoming a Demon Venerable? But I’m not confident I can defeat Senior Brother.”

“You must become a Demon Venerable.”

“Why?”

“If your Senior Brother becomes a Demon Venerable, he won’t leave you alive.”

“Senior Brother… isn’t that kind of person.”

“You hesitated just now, didn’t you? Do you know why? You hesitated because you know that he is exactly that kind of person.”

“……”

“The moment your name was raised as a candidate to succeed the next Demon Venerable, you stepped onto a dueling platform from which you could descend only after fighting a life-or-death battle.”

Sa Ujong sincerely wished for Cheongseon to become a Demon Venerable.

If that happened, he would be able to manipulate the enormous power of the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable from behind the scenes. The mere thought made his heart race.

And at the end of this thirst for power still lurked his twisted desire. To become stronger, and stronger still.

‘Someday, I’ll make the Sword Venerable my woman.’

Cheongseon quietly watched Sa Ujong’s eyes as he stared blankly at the portrait of the Ilhwa Sword Venerable hanging on the wall.

“What are you thinking about so deeply?”

“How can I put you on the seat of Demon Venerable?”

“There’s no way at present.”

“There is. One way.”

“What way?”

“The Pungcheon Cult Leader is here right now. If you receive his instruction, you can defeat your Senior Brother.”

“That’s ridiculous! You know perfectly well that one’s martial arts don’t improve that quickly.”

“You were born with extraordinary talent, weren’t you? Besides, your opponent isn’t just anyone—it’s the Pungcheon Cult Leader. Even if he teaches you only a single secret technique, you should be able to defeat your Senior Brother.”

“If I become the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s disciple, the other Demon Venerables will hate me.”

“This is a matter of life and death. You have to survive first and think about the rest afterward.”

Sa Ujong pressed himself against her.

He did not stop until she finally answered that she understood.

* * *

“Those despicable bastards! I never imagined they could be this despicable.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader could not contain his anger.

“And you’re not bothered by this?”

The man in shackles merely stared at Eumroe Jong without a word.

“Say something.”

“Didn’t you anticipate this? That they would try to send us back by any means necessary?”

“I did. Yes, I did. But I never thought they would use such a filthy method. Does this make any sense? Playing games with our food?”

According to the report that had just arrived, the meals and side dishes provided to his subordinates waiting in the Outer Court had changed. The portions had shrunk, and the quality had deteriorated. High-quality meat had been replaced with chunks of fat, and the liquor with cheap swill.

“Who would believe that the Heavenly Demon Divine Church would stoop to tampering with someone’s food? They might as well feed us poison!”

What made matters worse was that the people eating this food were none other than the Pungcheon Cult’s main forces: the Ten Great Demon Warriors and the hundred Blood Fury Army soldiers. He could not simply sit by and do nothing—not when he was too ashamed to face them.

“I should go confront the Cult Leader.”

“What are you going to say? That you want your food back?”

“Of course I should! I’ll shout that your damned subordinate stole all the food meant for my men. I’ll roll around on the floor of the Heavenly Demon Hall and complain that I’ve never seen anything so despicable in my life.”

“Then have a safe trip.”

Of course, the Pungcheon Cult Leader did not do that. Instead, his arrow of anger turned elsewhere.

“What is that blowhard, the Second Young Master, doing? Didn’t he say he’d find a way?”

“Wait patiently.”

“I’m saying this because I can’t be patient right now.”

“Those who respect the Cult Leader will respect him even if they go hungry. Those who dislike him will dislike him even if they are served a sumptuous feast for all three meals. So there’s no need to worry about it.”

“Things in this world don’t proceed as simply as that. Those who respect me will feel betrayed, saying, ‘Even though I respect him this much, he’s starving me?’ And those who dislike me will drive a wedge between everyone by saying, ‘I knew that pathetic fool would end up in a situation like this.’”

“Then retain only the people who can endure every storm.”

“Damn it! Damn!”

At that moment, word came from outside that Cheongseon had arrived.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader looked at the man in shackles in surprise.

The man in shackles wore an expression of complete understanding and gave a faint smile.

Cheongseon soon entered and offered a respectful greeting.

“It is an honor to meet the revered Supreme Lord Beyond the Passes. I am Cheongseon of Seohwanjin.”

“I’ve heard much about you. You’re said to be the greatest master of sorcery in Seohwanjin?”

“Your words are too generous. I apologize for not coming to see you sooner.”

“No need. I’m grateful that you came at all.”

“I once heard from my late master that the roots of the martial arts I learned lay in the Blood Cult.”

“That is correct. In that sense, we can call ourselves fellow disciples. So, have you received the full inheritance of martial arts from the Demon Venerable?”

“Unfortunately, I have not. That is why I have come to ask you this. Please accept me as your disciple!”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader glanced at the man in shackles and sent him a transmission.

―The Second Young Master is insane.

―This is why we have to remain the Second Young Master until the very end.

―Damn it! Just thinking about him coming to claim my divine artifact is already making my stomach churn.

―Let’s take this opportunity to broaden that narrow-mindedness of yours.

―Shut up!

Though such a conversation passed between them inwardly, the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s expression remained solemn beyond measure.

“Seeing the martial arts of the lands beyond the passes shine brightly in the Central Plains is something I sincerely wish for. However, I do not know whether your Cult Leader will permit this precious connection between us.”

“As far as I know, our church has no restrictions regarding the formation of a master-disciple relationship. Moreover, this will ultimately make Seohwanjin stronger, so the Cult Leader will not oppose it.”

“Are you certain? Your Senior Brother will make an issue of this.”

“Teach me. Teach me how to defeat my Senior Brother.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader spoke quietly to her.

“Your Senior Brother may kill you.”

Cheongseon raised her head and looked at the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

The look in her eyes was not that of a passive woman being manipulated by a man. The innocent woman who had shared a bed with Sa Ujong and the woman standing here now were clearly different people.

Sa Ujong did not know.

He did not know that her docility in bed was merely a tiny part of who she was.

He thought that her feelings for him came from affection, when in truth they stemmed from her ferocious self-love—a self-love that would never yield what she considered hers. He did not know that she was a cruel woman who would destroy something if she could not possess it. Sa Ujong knew nothing.

Cheongseon smiled sweetly and said,

“But I can’t exactly let myself die, can I?”

Reading in her eyes the raw madness that only villains could possess, the Pungcheon Cult Leader smiled with satisfaction.

“I find myself looking forward to meeting the next Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable.”

Chapter 70: The Difference in Thoroughness.
-------------------------------------------
Cheongseon performed the rite of nine bows (九拜之禮) and formally became the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s disciple.

The news spread in an instant, and Seohwanjin and the rest of the cult began to stir.

Unexpectedly, there were more who supported her, saying it had been a good choice, than there were those who cursed her.

They thought it wiser for her to learn properly under the Pungcheon Cult Leader and ascend to the seat of Demon Venerable, rather than uphold justification or save face only to take the position while her demonic arts were weak. As expected, the demonic practitioners of their cult pursued practical benefit more than pretext.

In particular, the sorcerers of Seohwanjin, whom everyone had expected to oppose her fiercely, came forward in even stronger support.

Because of this, First Disciple Yangdo found himself in an awkward position. His plan to worsen public opinion by saying Cheongseon was receiving outside help had gone awry.

Now, no one could tell who would become the successor.

“What do you think of her?”

The one the Pungcheon Cult Leader was curious about was Cheongseon.

“Aren’t you the one who sees people well, Cult Leader? Trust your own judgment.”

“She seemed to have more backbone than she looked.”

“I saw her similarly.”

“She might be worth raising.”

He had not taken her as a disciple because he had wanted one in the first place. She had merely been an excuse to remain in the Central Plains, but once he actually saw Cheongseon, the strange malice she exuded appealed to him.

“I suppose there’s no helping the kind of man I am. I see a wicked bitch, and I’m drawn to her.”

“Be careful. I saw ambition in her that would devour even her master.”

“That’s why I like her.”

When the Pungcheon Cult Leader grinned, the shackled man also smiled faintly.

“You seem to be in a good mood lately.”

“Me?”

“You hardly curse, either.”

Above all, he had not asked him for several days to open a new space with the Spacetime Transposition Art.

“If you are bored, shall I curse for you a little?”

“Not at all! I like things as they are now.”

But deep down, a part of him missed the shackled man’s curses.

“By the way, the cult leader on this side is quiet.”

It seemed the Pungcheon Cult Leader was bothered by the Heavenly Demon.

“He will remain so.”

“Why do you think that?”

“Because the Second Young Master must have met his father and settled the matter.”

“Would he have thought that far ahead?”

“He would have. Success and failure come down to the difference in thoroughness.”

“Why are you so favorable toward the Second Young Master? Your evaluations are generous, too. Did he slip you some money?”

The shackled man raised his head and looked at the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“Do you dislike that?”

“I do.”

“Then let us return beyond the frontier. If we go somewhere the Second Young Master is not, there will be no reason for me to show him favor, will there?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader was about to get irritated, but instead merely let out a sigh. Annoying as it was, it was not wrong. After all, he was the one who had dragged this man all the way here, clanking in chains.

“I told you, did I not? You are not blessed enough to have everything, Cult Leader.”

“Damn it. Stop with that already! Why do you keep saying I’m ill-fated?”

“I say it because you keep trying to turn toward the truly ill-fated path.”

“You never lose a single word.”

It was then. A subordinate came and reported a new fact.

“Cooks came to our warriors in the outer compound and prepared food for them.”

“Cooks? What cooks?”

“They say they were sent by the Second Young Master. They set out a lavish feast with the finest ingredients and left.”

At those words, the Pungcheon Cult Leader looked at the shackled man and asked,

“Is this it? This difference in thoroughness you spoke of?”

The shackled man nodded.

“He is truly remarkable for his young age. I did say it, but even I did not expect him to take care of things to this extent. Perhaps……”

“Perhaps?”

“Perhaps this is the moment an unparalleled figure is being born in the Demonic Cult—no, in the murim. And we are sharing that moment in history.”

“Just because he sent some cooks?”

“Yes. He sends thieves, sends disciples, and sends cooks. I am sure of it. A moment in history.”

Unlike the impassioned shackled man, the Pungcheon Cult Leader was indifferent.

“And in that moment in history, I am having all my sacred relics taken from me.”

As if he already knew that was the reason, the shackled man calmly consoled the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“Change your perspective. Your sacred relics are being put to useful purpose in accomplishing something great.”

“As I said before, am I not absent as the protagonist of that great work? I have no desire to become a tool for someone else’s success.”

“If you wish to become the protagonist, you must be as diligent as the Second Young Master. You must make effort like the Second Young Master as well.”

“I make effort too, don’t I?”

“The effort of asking me about every single thing?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader could not refute him. These days, he had left most matters to the shackled man to handle.

Seeing the Pungcheon Cult Leader still unable to escape his anguish, the shackled man’s expression and tone changed.

“Cult Leader.”

At that moment, the Pungcheon Cult Leader was incredibly glad for his informal speech. His condescension sounded so good that he had even been worried about what he would do if the man never spoke down to him again.

“I know you make effort too.”

“What effort do I make? You’re right.”

“No. Holding back your greed and handing over your sacred relics is effort as well. Gathering those sacred relics was your effort, and protecting them was effort too. I know very well that even your greed is your effort. I know that your effort is as difficult as the effort of someone escaping a heavenly net and earthly snare (天羅地網), drenched in blood.”

Emotion welled up in the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s heart. Only recently had he come to feel how much comfort it gave a person when someone understood their heart.

Still, he did not want to show weakness before the shackled man, so the Pungcheon Cult Leader forced himself to hide his true feelings and shouted,

“I hate it! I don’t want to give up my sacred relics! That damned Second Young Master, just let him come. I’ll tell him to get out at once. Get lost, Second Young Master! Cover your ears, because my roar will burst your eardrums. Understand?”

Just then, a subordinate outside said,

“The Second Young Master has arrived.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader shook his head with an incredulous expression.

“That insane Second Young Master! He really appears at a moment like this?”

The shackled man laughed aloud for the first time in a long while. Covering his ears, he said politely,

“Now then, I am ready.”

* * *

“Welcome, Second Young Master.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader greeted me with a pleasant face.

On days when he gave me a sacred relic, his hands had visibly trembled, but today he looked somewhat at ease. Had he perhaps attained the truth that giving up makes one comfortable?

A short while later, a subordinate brought in a drinking table.

“Let us have a drink together today.”

“Gladly.”

“Take a cup.”

I accepted the wine.

“Now, drink.”

I clinked cups with him in good spirits and drank. Of course, there was a Poison-Repelling Pearl held in my mouth.

“It pleases me to see you trusting me.”

I did not go out of my way to spit out the Poison-Repelling Pearl and offend him.

It was right then.

“By any chance, are you holding a Poison-Repelling Pearl in your mouth?”

I knew. The shackled man had sent a sound transmission to tell him I was holding one. As expected, he was no ordinary man.

“Yes, I am.”

I spat the Poison-Repelling Pearl from my mouth.

“Good heavens! When did you put that in?”

“When the drinking table was brought in.”

“There was no sign of it at all.”

“I practiced a great deal.”

“A Poison-Repelling Pearl in wine I gave you…… Truly, that is too much.”

“Did I not tell you before? There is still no bond between us. The phrase ‘between you and me,’ Cult Leader, does not exist in this world.”

“You say that even after coming here today to take another one of my precious sacred relics?”

“If a bond is to form amid this chaos, it will not be determined by how many sacred relics I take.”

“Just as, right now, the only thing sitting between us is a lonely Poison-Repelling Pearl?”

“A definite relationship where we give what must be given and receive what must be received is better than a flimsy bond that may change depending on circumstances.”

“I truly cannot beat you with words. Just like someone else.”

“Pardon?”

“Nothing.”

I guessed that the person he meant was the shackled man.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader rose.

“Well then, since the taste of wine has been ruined, let us choose a sacred relic. Go on, choose quickly.”

“Thank you.”

Before choosing a sacred relic, I went to the Yin Thunder Bell.

“I will look at the Yin Thunder Bell once more. No matter how often I see it, it is magnificent.”

In truth, it was because I wanted to see the shackled man up close again.

While looking at the Yin Thunder Bell, I glanced at the man. The shackled man sat with his head lowered, like a fossil.

‘Why are you helping me?’

I also liked that he could have sent me a sound transmission, but did not. He was not a frivolous man.

Would the day ever come when I could speak with this man? How far were his fate and mine intertwined?

I moved again and walked toward the place where the sacred relics were kept.

I acted as if I were looking this way and that, but I had already decided what to take.

“I will choose this.”

The sacred relic I chose this time was a thickly rolled bandage.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s expression hardened. Instead of words, his face cursed like this:

Damn it! Curse you! How do you only pick out the precious ones like this?

“Do you know what that is and choose it?”

Of course, I knew all too well.

“I do not. Cloth wrapped up like this simply looked somehow mysterious.”

“That is……”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader took a deep breath. After painstakingly calming the anger rising within him, he explained it.

“That is Supreme Heavenly Silkworm Silk. It is an eternal treasure said not to be cut even when struck by a sword, and when wrapped in several layers, it is known to block even sword qi or sword aura. It can be used by wrapping it around the arms or legs, or by wrapping it around the heart to protect it. And that is not all. It wards off cold and does not burn in fire. In the murim, the weapons that can cut this Supreme Heavenly Silkworm Silk without sword qi or sword aura can be counted on one hand.”

Included among those few weapons was the Black Demon Sword at my waist.

“My knowledge is shallow, so I failed to recognize that it was such a precious item.”

“If you failed to recognize it, why of all things did you choose this? Why, exactly!”

“I apologize.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader could not hide how pained he was to part with it. After sighing several times, he handed it to me. His hand trembled.

“Please put it to good use.”

“Yes, thank you.”

I took it as if wrenching it from him when he would not let go.

I felt gratitude toward the Pungcheon Cult Leader. Though his heart must have ached, he always kept his promises and gave me sacred relics without stint.

It was excessive compensation compared to what I had done for him, but the reason I accepted it so readily was that I intended to make his dream come true.

“I will never forget your goodwill, Cult Leader.”

At a time like this, it would have been nice if he had shown some magnanimity, but the Pungcheon Cult Leader could not hide his lingering regret.

Over his shoulder, the shackled man was looking at me. With how reluctant he was, there was no way the cult leader would have handed over the sacred relics so obediently. The Blood God Pill earlier had been the same, and so was this Supreme Heavenly Silkworm Silk. It was clear I had received them smoothly thanks to the shackled man.

What I sent in my gaze was this:

Thank you. I will not forget.

As if he understood, the shackled man slowly lowered his head and returned to being a fossil.

* * *

After returning to my quarters, I took out the Supreme Heavenly Silkworm Silk.

I unwound enough of it to wrap around my wrist about three times, then cut it with the Black Demon Sword.

It would likely have cut even as it was, but to make a clean cut, I infused internal energy and cut it carefully.

Before eternal treasured swords like Father’s Heavenly Demon Sword or my Black Demon Sword, it was merely cloth, but to other weapons, it became a monster.

I wrapped it around my left wrist in three layers.

No matter what happened, as long as I wore this, my hand would never be cut off by an opponent’s attack. For now, having it wrapped only around my left wrist was enough. My right hand would be protected by the Black Demon Sword. The fact that it could be used intensively only where needed was what made it better than any treasured robe or protective artifact.

I wrapped the rest of the Supreme Heavenly Silkworm Silk around the hilt of the Black Demon Sword. I would use it when the need arose next time.

I only hoped there would be no occasion where I had to wrap this all around my body and charge somewhere.

Because hell would unfold there.

Or perhaps that place would already be hell to begin with.

Chapter 71: Scheming with Everyone.
-----------------------------------
The Blood Heaven Blade Demon came to find me.

He had always waited near my residence with his great saber thrust into the ground, but these days he came directly to wherever I was. If I was at home, he came to my home; if I was in my office, like today, he came to my office.

I am your elder, so you should come to me. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had never had that sort of authoritarianism in him from the start. He was the sort of man who came when he wished to come and left when he wished to leave.

“Did you hear that Seo Hwanjin’s third disciple became the disciple of the Pungcheon Cult Leader?”

“Yes, I heard.”

“That was your doing, wasn’t it?”

“Why is it that whenever something new happens, you assume I did it?”

“So, was it not?”

“It was.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon raised his voice as if to say, Of course it was.

“What on earth are you plotting with the Pungcheon Cult Leader?”

“What grand plot? I’m simply helping the man.”

“You truly mean to have the Pungcheon Cult advance into the Central Plains?”

“Is that not allowed?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked with a serious expression.

“Can you handle it?”

“If you mean the Pungcheon Cult, do I not have you, Elder? If you will not fight the Demon Venerable, then please at least deal with the Pungcheon Cult Leader.”

“He is not someone to look down on. Though he came to our cult almost as if dragged here, he is the man who leads the martial world beyond the borders. If the martial world beyond the borders goes berserk, the Central Plains will become a sea of blood.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was serious, and I took his warning sincerely.

“I will keep that in mind.”

Thinking of the Pungcheon Cult Leader as an extraordinary man naturally brought the shackled man to mind. He was the one controlling and guiding that extraordinary Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“But why Cheongseon?”

“She was easy to move.”

“Can Cheongseon become the Demon Venerable in the future?”

“As I see it, the first disciple and the third disciple are six of one and half a dozen of the other. No matter which of the two it is, it will take quite a long time for them to properly take on the form of a true Demon Venerable.”

As if he agreed with that point, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon nodded.

“So you should raise Investigator Seo well, Elder.”

“Tell him to wake from his dream.”

“I am the one dreaming. Investigator Seo does not dream such dreams.”

“Stop meddling in other people’s affairs and tend to your own.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who had been about to rise from his seat, looked at the supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk wrapped around my wrist and asked,

“What is that?”

“I wrapped it there for style.”

“You received it from the Pungcheon Cult Leader, didn’t you?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon immediately realized that it was no ordinary object.

“Why do you think that?”

“It is something I have never seen before, and you would not help the Pungcheon Cult Leader without compensation.”

“Wouldn’t it be too miserly to expect a reward every time I help with something?”

“So? Did you not receive one?”

“I did.”

“You miserly wretch!”

I laughed, and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon shook his head as if there was no stopping me.

“This is supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk.”

“Ah! So this is that thing.”

“As expected, you know of it.”

“This is my first time seeing it in person.”

“Shall I cut some off for you?”

I made as if to immediately unwind the supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk wrapped around my sword hilt.

“No need. It is not something that suits this old body.”

“Isn’t it precisely because your body is old that you should wrap it around yourself?”

“No need.”

He had no authoritarianism, nor any greed for possessions. At times like this, I truly thought that perhaps his enjoyment of reading poetry and paintings was his essence.

“Did you receive anything else besides this?”

“I also received the Blood God Pill.”

I was honest with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. I had been until now, and I intended to be so in the future as much as possible. Because I believed that this honesty would become the key to completely opening the door to his heart.

“The Blood God Pill?”

Perhaps because I had said it too casually, it took the Blood Heaven Blade Demon a little time to recognize what the Blood God Pill was.

His expression changed in this order. What was the Blood God Pill again? The Blood God Pill? Huh? Surely not that Blood God Pill? This is madness! The Blood God Pill?

Startled, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon sprang up from his seat.

“The greatest spirit pill beyond the borders?”

“Yes.”

“You mean the Pungcheon Cult Leader handed that over?”

He was so shocked that, for a moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon could not continue speaking.

“Where is it?”

“In my stomach.”

“Good heavens! Your internal energy must now have surpassed mine.”

I only smiled. In truth, even before taking the Blood God Pill, with the Demon Essence Pill and the Heavenly Beyond Divine Pill, my internal energy had been enough to stand shoulder to shoulder with the Demon Venerables. Now that I had taken the Blood God Pill as well, in terms of internal energy, I would overwhelm them.

“Do not worry. Even so, you are still my left wing, Elder.”

“Hmph! You received a spirit pill more effective than the Heavenly Beyond Divine Pill and even supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk, so I suppose you will tear off something like the left wing.”

“My left wing is one with my body, so it cannot be replaced. If it is torn off, I die.”

“As I said before, if the body dies, the wing will fly off to find a new body.”

“I shall pray it meets a better body.”

The words passed between us like jokes, but before I knew it, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s expression had softened.

“You were not perhaps jealous just now, were you?”

“Jealous? As if! It was a warning. I must do this much to make it clear that you cannot covet my position just because someone gave you a sacred treasure.”

“Confirmed.”

“I am truly leaving. Tend to your work.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon rose from his seat. Just as he was about to leave, he suddenly said,

“I… would not mind even if it were a dragonfly’s wing.”

Meaning that it would be fine even if I attached several wings.

Those words made emotion well up in my chest. With such tolerance, how could I ever treat the Blood Heaven Blade Demon lightly?

It was just as he was about to leave. A subordinate entered and announced another visitor.

“The One Blossom Sword Venerable has come.”

At the news that the Sword Venerable had arrived, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was startled.

“Did you call her here to make her meet me?”

“How can you say that, when you yourself came here all of a sudden?”

Flustered, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon opened the window. I stopped him from trying to leave through it.

“That is far too undignified.”

“She will dislike seeing me.”

He was still awkward and uncomfortable when it came to his relationship with the One Blossom Sword Venerable.

“What does that matter? Since when did you care so much about others?”

After standing by the window and agonizing for a moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon returned to his seat.

In the meantime, the One Blossom Sword Venerable came inside.

Seeing the Blood Heaven Blade Demon in the office, this time she was the one who was startled.

“You? You did not deliberately arrange this meeting, did you?”

I smiled and said to her,

“How are the two of you so alike? How would I know when the two of you would come and arrange such a meeting? Both of you came without even sending word. If you must complain, complain to the one up there.”

As I said that, I looked at the sky beyond the window.

Even without my having to say, Is this not all fate? the two of them understood what I meant.

They were two people I wished to bring together even by force, and seeing them meet so naturally like this made me think that perhaps the heavens truly were arranging their reconciliation.

“Now, please sit.”

The One Blossom Sword Venerable sat facing the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“What brings you here, Senior?”

“I came because there is something I must tell you.”

At that, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon rose from his seat.

“Speak with him. I shall be going.”

“It is something you may hear as well.”

She would surely be uncomfortable being with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, and yet she stopped him. That meant it concerned all of us and was important enough for that. Indeed, what she revealed was exactly that.

“The Demon Buddha came to see me and asked me for a favor.”

“What favor?”

“He asked me to support Yang Do, the first disciple among the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s two disciples.”

The selection of a new Demon Venerable was entirely under the authority of the Eight Demon Venerables. If a Demon Venerable died without naming a successor, the remaining Demon Venerables would make recommendations and choose the next Demon Venerable. In the end, it meant one could only ascend to the seat of Demon Venerable by receiving the support of more than half of the Demon Venerables.

“You said this before, did you not? That as your sparring friend, it would be good if I supported someone on our side.”

“You remembered.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, on the other hand, looked curious as to what that meant.

“Sparring friend? What does that mean?”

“Such a thing happened.”

“You are conspiring with everyone except me, it seems.”

“How could that be? You are my first wing, Elder.”

This time, the One Blossom Sword Venerable asked,

“Wing?”

“Elder is my left wing. And I am currently searching earnestly for my right wing.”

I deliberately stared straight at the One Blossom Sword Venerable. Reading my wish for her to become my right wing, the One Blossom Sword Venerable quickly changed the subject.

“So? Whom do you support?”

“Cheongseon.”

“I had a feeling that would be the case. I thought your will would differ from the Demon Buddha’s. Very well, I too will support Cheongseon.”

Even setting aside her relationship with the Demon Buddha, going against another Demon Venerable’s request would be quite burdensome. And yet she was readily willing to support me.

She was also showing consideration for me. I could tell just from the fact that she had asked the Blood Heaven Blade Demon to listen with us. This matter required both of their help.

“Thank you.”

I was sincerely grateful to her. The same was true of the help she had given me last time regarding Sa Ujong.

Very well, Senior. Let us be sparring friends for life.

She did not even ask why it had to be Cheongseon. That meant she trusted me that much.

At that moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked me,

“But even so, that is only two votes from us, is it not? Have you secured the votes of the other Demon Venerables?”

To place Cheongseon in the seat of Demon Venerable, we needed four votes among the seven Demon Venerables.

“Not yet.”

“What do you plan to do?”

I spoke politely to the two of them.

“Please each persuade just one person. I ask this of you.”

“If Cheongseon becomes the Demon Venerable, what benefit is there for us?”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s question, I answered shamelessly,

“Rather than the two of you, it would benefit me.”

“It should benefit me!”

“What benefits me is the same as benefiting you, Elder.”

Of course, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was only blustering for no reason, though he intended to grant my request.

On the other hand, the One Blossom Sword Venerable readily accepted my request.

“I will try to persuade the other Demon Venerables for your sake. If it goes well, at least one person will cast a vote for Cheongseon.”

Suddenly, I thought that perhaps this favor of hers was not simply because of our sparring. It was too great a favor for that.

Looking at her, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said with a sulky face,

“If you act like that, it makes me look like I have nothing.”

He had thrown the words out in his own way to ease the awkward atmosphere between them, but the One Blossom Sword Venerable answered him with a stern face.

“Were you not a person who had nothing to begin with?”

In an instant, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s expression hardened. The pleasant atmosphere cooled in the blink of an eye.

Their gazes collided in midair. They fought with their eyes and did not yield an inch.

I did not stop them and gave them enough time to expend their emotions. This process had to be repeated again and again. After all, they would not kill each other.

The first to turn his head away was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. The One Blossom Sword Venerable, the victor of the staring contest, snorted. Fortunately, the time it took for their conflict to dissolve was growing shorter and shorter.

I smiled at the One Blossom Sword Venerable and expressed my gratitude.

“Thank you for informing me about the Demon Buddha’s matter. I am even more grateful that you are willing to persuade the Demon Venerables. I will never forget this favor, Senior.”

“What favor? I will see you next time.”

The One Blossom Sword Venerable answered with a smile and left the office first.

After she left, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon also soon rose from his seat.

“I shall go as well.”

There was the matter of my receiving a sacred treasure from the Pungcheon Cult Leader, and also the matter of being sparring friends with the One Blossom Sword Venerable. It would have been natural for such things to bother him, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon did not show it at all.

“You are always my first priority, Elder.”

“Your words alone are smooth.”

When I gave an awkward smile, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon also chuckled.

“Even if the Sword Venerable left so confidently, persuading another Demon Venerable will not be easy. The Demon Buddha’s side will also be moving diligently.”

“Please help me too, Elder.”

“Will it work when I am being ostracized among the Demon Venerables?”

Though he said that, his back looked more dependable than anyone’s.

If the two of them each persuaded one person, our side could make four votes. As for how things would turn out, even I found it difficult to predict.

I decided simply to trust the two of them. Both the Blade Demon and the Sword Venerable had lived for decades as Demon Venerables. I would trust the lives they had lived.

In any case, the two of them were doing something difficult for my sake, so I could not simply hand the fruits of it over to the Pungcheon Cult Leader for nothing.

I headed to the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s quarters. However, this time, my goal was not a sacred treasure. I intended to lay the first stone in order to obtain something even more precious than a sacred treasure.

Chapter 72: Must Be Nice to Be the Later Waves.
-----------------------------------------------
Cult Leader Pungcheon looked at the place where the divine relics had been set. He looked and looked again; even after closing his eyes and opening them, the spots where the Blood God Pill and the supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk had been were empty.

“I can’t bear to look at it. She was a flawless beauty, and now she looks as if her two front teeth have been knocked out.”

At Cult Leader Pungcheon’s lament, the shackled man looked at the Yin Thunder Bell and spoke quietly.

“She still has her fangs and her molars. She can tear into her opponent, and she can clench her molars and steel her resolve.”

“You get excited every time a divine relic disappears, don’t you?”

“How did you know? If you unfasten these shackles, I might even dance.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon let out a sigh.

“To be honest, I’m afraid.”

“What are you afraid of?”

“That all of this will turn into a vain dream. That when I wake, nothing will remain. Then I’ll regret it, won’t I? What in the world possessed me to give up so much?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon looked at the shackled man. Then the shackled man raised his head and swept back the hair that hung down in thick locks, showing his face for the first time in a long while.

“Why the sudden gesture?”

“I’m showing you the man who possessed you and made you give up everything.”

“You madman.”

The man was right. What had bewitched him now was not the Second Young Master. It was that shackled man. That man who boldly said he had bewitched him, who smiled as he said he would destroy him.

“How do I make Cheongseon a Demon Venerable?”

“We must defeat the Demon Venerables one by one and draw them in. Conciliate them, persuade them, recruit them. At least two divine relics will go into it. If our luck is bad, we may need one more.”

It seemed the shackled man had already finished his calculations, but Cult Leader Pungcheon’s face twisted into a deep frown.

“You’re saying in a roundabout way that I should invest in the Second Young Master, aren’t you? For you, it’s the Second Young Master to the very end.”

“How long do you intend to lean on the Second Young Master? Act like a man and move and deal with it yourself. I will tell you which approach to the Demon Venerables would be advantageous.”

“I told you not to be sarcastic!”

“You are right. Then just do nothing and remain still.”

“Is that something a strategist should say?”

No sooner had Cult Leader Pungcheon spoken than there came the clank of chains.

At that silent protest—what strategist in the world wore such shackles?—Cult Leader Pungcheon could say nothing.

“I say this because you think of me as your strategist. Just stay still. Sometimes, the greatest results are obtained by doing nothing.”

After a brief pause, Cult Leader Pungcheon asked,

“Can I really do that?”

“The Second Young Master will come.”

“And then?”

“He will demand a divine relic in exchange for placing Cheongseon in the seat of Demon Venerable.”

“Even for the Second Young Master, will that be possible?”

“We have to believe. We have already crossed a bridge from which there is no return.”

Just then, Cheongseon, who had been training in the yard, came to deliver news.

“The Second Young Master has come to visit.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon was startled, and at the same time impressed. Because things were moving just as the shackled man had predicted, he felt the illusion that he held the upper hand.

“He’s a fellow who’ll never become a proper gentleman.”

“It is because we are always thinking about the Second Young Master and talking only of the Second Young Master.”

“I told you to stop talking about him! Stop taking his side! Your master is me, me!”

“Then who told you to become the front wave of the Yangtze?”

At the words front wave of the Yangtze, Cult Leader Pungcheon’s heart dropped. The shackled man had likely said it without any special meaning, but to Cult Leader Pungcheon it struck hard. These days, he had already been feeling as if he were being pushed by something, swept toward a place he did not wish to go.

“Fine, you must be pleased, being the rear wave of the Yangtze.”

When he had decided to come to the Central Plains, he had never dreamed he would be having such a conversation with the shackled man.

‘Truly, one can never know what lies even an inch ahead in life.’

For the first time, Cult Leader Pungcheon felt the unfamiliar tension that an unpredictable life could give. Put another way, it also meant he had lived a life that was that predictable and smooth.

* * *

When I went to see Cult Leader Pungcheon, Cheongseon was there as well.

She had been in the middle of training in the yard, and when she recognized me, she greeted me respectfully.

“I greet the Second Young Master.”

When the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable had been alive, ours had been a relationship as indifferent as looking at chickens and dogs, but now the situation had changed. She needed outside supporters who would back her, and she needed to make a good impression on me, the Second Young Master and Pavilion Master of the Yellow Springs Pavilion.

“I heard that you became Cult Leader Pungcheon’s disciple, Lady Cheong. Congratulations.”

“Thank you, Second Young Master.”

She would not know. That things had flowed this way had begun with my leaking information to Sa Ujong.

“I came because I have news to inform the Cult Leader of. Lady Cheong, you should hear it with us.”

When we entered, the shackled man slightly raised his head, and our eyes met in passing. Since last time, we had been greeting each other in our own way.

“What is it?”

“The Demon Buddha is making his move.”

“The Demon Buddha? How?”

“He is persuading the Demon Venerables in order to make his first disciple, Yang Do, the next Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable.”

“Damn it! To think a friend would go even further.”

“Now that the Demon Buddha has begun moving in earnest, I think it will be difficult for Lady Cheong here to become a Demon Venerable.”

Cheongseon bit her lip gently. She had taken a great risk in order to become a Demon Venerable. If she failed to become one even after becoming Cult Leader Pungcheon’s disciple, she might ultimately be driven beyond the borders alongside Cult Leader Pungcheon.

“Second Young Master. Is there no way to seat this child in the position of Demon Venerable? As you know, in this Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, I cannot move.”

“There is a way, but it will not be easy.”

“What way?”

“We must move Demon Venerables as well. Fortunately, the two elders, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable, are well disposed toward me. If I ask those two, perhaps…”

“Please help us.”

“Please help me, Second Young Master.”

Cheongseon was just as desperate as Cult Leader Pungcheon.

“This is not as easy as it sounds.”

I put on an act that was not entirely an act.

“I do not know what the Demon Venerables who agree to give their votes will demand. Even if things go well, I will end up owing a great debt to the Saber Demon and the Sword Venerable.”

“Are my disciple and I not the ones receiving help? I will never forget that merit.”

“Very well. I will try.”

“Thank you. Please wait a moment.”

As if he had something to say to me privately, Cult Leader Pungcheon sent Cheongseon out.

“You go outside and train.”

“Yes, Master.”

After greeting me, Cheongseon went out. Her gaze toward me contained desperation.

Once she had gone, Cult Leader Pungcheon asked,

“What do you want this time? It must be a divine relic again, yes?”

“No.”

“No?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon was astonished.

“The only thing more important than a divine relic is my life.”

He said it like a joke, but it was a statement that contained his true feelings. From my mouth flowed words that must have sounded like a blessing to such a man.

“This time, there is nothing I want.”

“Nothing?”

“Yes, nothing.”

“Truly?”

When I nodded as if to make it certain, Cult Leader Pungcheon’s expression brightened.

“When the result comes out, I will treat you to a drink.”

“On top of that, even wine?”

“You gave me two precious divine relics, did you not? Win or lose, let us have a drink.”

“Where?”

“We should drink here. With the divine relics stolen, if the Cult Leader leaves his quarters, everyone will find it strange. I will prepare wine and dishes and bring them.”

Of course, that was not the reason. It was so I could drink where the shackled man was.

“I will do my best to make sure it becomes a victory drink, not a drink in defeat.”

With those words, I rose from my seat. Cult Leader Pungcheon was pleased, but at the same time he was clearly flustered.

“Just in case, teach your disciple well. Lady Cheong may have to spar with the first disciple, Yang Do.”

“I have that in mind as well.”

“Then I will take my leave.”

I was about to leave like that, but then I turned back to Cult Leader Pungcheon.

“Ah, there is one thing I would like to ask.”

“What is it?”

“I tried to create a sky in the space made by the Space-Time Transposition Art, but the mnemonic formula did not connect smoothly. May I ask you to explain it to me once more?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon was startled. The shackled man sitting there also flinched in surprise. He was someone who rarely showed emotion, but this time there was a reaction.

“To fill the space, you must succeed in creating it within half a shichen.”

“I have already succeeded in creating it within half a shichen.”

“What did you say?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon was shocked. When I had said before that I could open it in two shichen, he had not believed me. I had said I would show him directly next time, and yet I had already shortened it to half a shichen, so his surprise was only natural.

“Show me. Then I will tell you.”

“You will wait half a shichen?”

“I will wait.”

“Very well.”

I immediately began reciting the mnemonic formula and using the Space-Time Transposition Art.

Half a shichen later, we were inside the space I had made. I had deliberately brought the shackled man into it with us as well.

Cult Leader Pungcheon was surprised, and the shackled man was surprised too. A sky had spread out, but holes had opened here and there, making it a strange sky.

After looking around for a moment, Cult Leader Pungcheon asked me,

“Why did you make the sky first, of all things?”

“I like looking at the sky. When I look at it, it feels as if the tightness in my chest clears away. Then, please explain it to me.”

“I promised, so I will teach you.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon explained in detail the part of the Space-Time Transposition Art’s mnemonic formula that dealt with filling the space.

In truth, I could create the sky perfectly. But I had pretended not to be able to do it properly so that I could naturally show my ability directly in front of them.

After hearing Cult Leader Pungcheon’s explanation, half a shichen later, I opened a new space where a clear blue sky stretched high and wide, surprising the two of them even more.

It was not a move I had shown for Cult Leader Pungcheon’s sake. It was to show the shackled man directly what kind of talent I possessed.

Was I showing off? Yes. It was intentional boasting.

In my previous life, the foremost virtue I had felt a leader needed was strength. No matter what anyone said, it was strength. A person strong enough that one could trust and entrust everything to him.

And I saw it. The shackled man’s face as he looked up at the sky. His eyes, clear and yet lonely.

I still could not know what kind of person he was. But one thing was certain.

Though I called him the shackled man, he was a person whose appearance and air did not suit shackles at all.

* * *

After Geom Mugeuk left, Cult Leader Pungcheon and the shackled man were silent for a while.

The first to speak was Cult Leader Pungcheon.

“I saw a martial arts genius today.”

“He is certainly a Heavenly Martial Physique.”

“A Heavenly Martial Physique? Yes, without that, there is no way he could learn the Space-Time Transposition Art this quickly.”

“Even so, do you still find it a waste?”

“What? Giving him the Space-Time Transposition Art? Of course it is a waste. Is that feeling of waste not unrelated to the other party’s ability?”

“Who knows what legend he may accomplish with the martial art the Cult Leader passed on to him?”

“As I always say, what use is that legend if it is not me?”

“Our Cult Leader is a man who does not know how to give up.”

“You are being sarcastic again.”

“No. I think it is good that you are honest.”

“It’s because it’s you! Because I’m in front of you that I’m honest. If I go elsewhere, I’m not such a soft person.”

Though he himself so often revealed his inner thoughts like this, the shackled man had not once revealed his own. Cult Leader Pungcheon knew better than anyone the reason for that. Unless he removed those shackles, he would never know the man’s true heart.

But even so, there would likewise never be a day when he removed those shackles.

‘I will not let anyone take you from me.’

Whether he knew that feeling or not, the shackled man only stared at the evil spirits engraved on the Yin Thunder Bell.

“Are the evil spirits speaking to you again?”

“They ask again why I live like this.”

“Tell them everyone lives like this. You damned evil spirit, everyone lives like this. What makes you so special that you ask such a thing?”

But Cult Leader Pungcheon did not know. At this moment, the man was saying something entirely different to the evil spirit.

He was speaking of the person who kept telling him to stop taking his side and stop talking about him. The evil spirit, which looked as tight-lipped as the Yin Thunder Bell itself, merely listened silently to his words.

Chapter 73: The Greater the Master, the Simpler the Answer.
-----------------------------------------------------------
After leaving the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s quarters, I went to find Ian.

She was still wholly devoted to her training, and her Flying Heaven Sword Art had advanced by leaps and bounds.

“Ian.”

“Yes.”

“After you receive the Divine Poison Purification Art later, you may leave whenever you wish.”

“What? Why are you suddenly saying that?”

When I left the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s residence thinking that shackles did not suit the shackled man, Ian suddenly came to mind.

Perhaps there were shackles fastened around her ankles as well.

After all, were shackles only the kind visible to the eye?
