At that moment, Seo Daeryong was startled. Tears welled up in the eyes that had been staring blankly up at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. It was the first time the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had called him his disciple.

“Master! Thank you, Master.”

Seo Daeryong’s voice was damp with emotion. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who hated this sort of atmosphere, thundered at him.

“What are you staring at? If you came to train, then run!”

“Yes! Master!”

Seo Daeryong began to run. Seeing that he even forgot to say goodbye to me, it seemed the Blood Heaven Blade Demon calling him his disciple had truly moved him.

I lightly flew up and sat on the branch beside him.

“You brat, it’ll break.”

“I’ll be as light as a feather.”

Even with two people sitting on it, the thin branch did not break.

“You did well.”

“Well, did I?”

“You saw how happy he was, didn’t you? So you shouldn’t put things off. Just say everything when the moment comes, take credit for everything, hate when you hate. That’s how it should be.”

“A young whelp is educating an old man now?”

“Yes. You need to learn. That way you can live while mingling with young whelps.”

“If only you couldn’t talk.”

We looked at each other and chuckled.

“If you were so worried about me, you should have said so.”

“I came because I thought you might make that bastard fly into a rage. When Extreme Evil gets angry, he truly loses his mind.”

“Have you seen it?”

“I saw it once. Truly, no one could stop him.”

Yes, I knew that sight too. The phrase “no one could stop him” fit perfectly.

“With you, I thought he might somehow go mad and rampage.”

“Why me?”

“Because you really do have a way of infuriating people. Why would the Extreme Evil Laughing Demon be any different?”

“Fortunately, it didn’t go that far today.”

“You were able to come out without bloodshed because you are the Cult Leader’s son. Otherwise, one of you would have died.”

“Thank you for worrying about me, elder.”

“What thanks? Let’s go.”

“The sun is setting over there. Let’s watch for a moment before we go.”

“Getting old is sorrowful enough. What sunset?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon lightly launched himself away and left.

Left alone, I sat on the tree branch and watched the setting sun for a while.

Suddenly, I recalled the Extreme Evil Laughing Demon’s final moments before my regression. The sight of his eyes glittering with the last flare of life.

—I don’t remember. Even when I was young and played tag with my friends, did I want to kill the one who was “it”? Since when was I like this?

—Do you regret it? Living wickedly.

—Regret? What regret? I regret not killing more before I go. Now that I’m actually about to go to hell, I’m scared, that’s all. Scared. Ah! It’s suffocating. Take off the mask for me… Thanks. First time, isn’t it? Seeing my face?

—You must’ve heard you were handsome when you were young. Why did you wear it your whole life?

—When I was young, my master chose me and asked. If I followed him, I would never be able to take off this mask for the rest of my life. Could I do that? In exchange, he said he would let me live a life where I could kill people to my heart’s content. I said yes without hesitation. Ah! Come to think of it, I was already like that back then. That’s why Master recognized me.

—After your master died, at least you had the chance to take off the mask, didn’t you?

—You’d know if you wore one too. Once you put it on, you can’t take it off. Because your heart feels at ease. Wearing the mask, killing, and killing again. Killing. Later, it was the mask that was killing people.

—Where do you think you’re going, blaming the mask? You killed every last one of them.

—You noticed? Hahaha, cough. It’s hard to really laugh. This mask became my symbol, so how could I take it off? Should I send a message throughout the martial world saying, “The Extreme Evil Laughing Demon will be removing his mask starting today”? Hey, Mucheol. Ah, Mucheol probably wasn’t your real name either. What’s your real name?

—……

—Did you ever think of me as a friend, even once?

—……

—You bastard. If you come to hell, I’ll kill you with my own hands then.

—Fine. Take all the revenge you want then.

—It’s been hurting so much since earlier. Draw the sword now, my friend.

—Farewell.

The Extreme Evil Laughing Demon died by my hand.

At the time, while gathering materials for the Great Art, I had no choice but to eliminate him. He was a villain who deserved to die, but at the very moment of his death, he died thinking of me as a friend.

I never called him a friend to the end. Perhaps, at least in that moment, I had been the greater villain.

* * *

Despite my sudden visit, Father willingly permitted a game.

Usually, we played baduk in silence, but today I spoke a great deal.

“…Some time ago, I met the Demon Buddha. I said things I didn’t mean, probed him, and also made a sincere effort to learn what kind of person he was.”

Father offered no reply and focused only on the board. Normally, he would have scolded me, asking why I was being so noisy, but today he remained still.

Father had noticed. He had realized his son had come because his heart was troubled and he wanted to talk about this and that.

I continued speaking.

“Today, I also met the Extreme Evil Laughing Demon. I dislike that man. Not because he supports my elder brother, but because killing people while smiling like that makes him seem like a madman.”

Father captured a stone and said,

“Make dead stones only here.”

He was telling me not to kill any more Demon Venerables. When the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable died, he had told me not to kill any more, and now he was emphasizing it again.

What if I had to kill the Extreme Evil Laughing Demon again? What if that, too, was fate? If so, he and I were bound by a truly tenacious tie.

I could only hope that would not happen in this life.

“Do I have the skill to kill the Extreme Evil Laughing Demon? In any case, the reason I’m going around meeting the Demon Venerables is…”

I went on speaking again. While he was listening so obediently, I intended to say everything I wanted to say.

“It was for my own sake, and also for my brother’s. At first, when we went hunting, I boasted confidently that I would make all the Demon Venerables kneel and line up behind me, but now that I’m actually facing them, it isn’t so easy. You were right, Father. Dealing with people is the hardest thing in the world.”

Father said nothing and only played baduk. It seemed he had decided to merely listen today.

“Please don’t misunderstand and think this side of me makes me unfit to be heir. If I go through all these worries in advance, I’ll actually be able to show a stronger side later.”

“This is a rare opportunity. Why don’t you curse your elder brother too?”

“I’m not going to curse him for a while. He has it hard, gliding around elegantly like a swan. Beneath the water, he’s working his feet nonstop, so it must be exhausting! He isn’t the type to come and confess like I do, after all.”

For an instant, I saw Father chuckle. It was an extremely subtle and swift change, but now nothing escaped my Divine Eye Art.

“As expected, chattering away is making the game more and more unfavorable for me. I’ll focus now.”

And so I concentrated on the baduk. As we entered the endgame, I asked,

“When I sometimes don’t know what to do, may I come see you?”

Father’s hand, which was about to place a stone, paused briefly in midair.

“Isn’t that against the rules? Asking the judge for the answer?”

“Don’t think of yourself as the judge. You can just think of it as giving me pointers.”

Father placed the stone down.

“Giving pointers is rather fun.”

He did not say yes or no directly, but it meant he permitted it.

I knew. Even if he treated me kindly like this, it was separate from choosing the heir. He was someone who could declare tomorrow, “Your elder brother is the heir.”

The result of the game was that I barely won by two houses.

“Your skill is improving more and more. Are you perhaps not letting Strategist Sima sleep? Now that would be against the rules.”

“Do you not have a strategist as well?”

“They say they can’t play baduk.”

“Where in the world is there a strategist who cannot play baduk?”

“Now that I think about it, you’re right. I’ve never even seen a strategist who couldn’t play baduk in storybooks. I must have been deceived by a strategist who can’t even play baduk.”

At my banter, Father chuckled.

“Then I will see you next month.”

I greeted Father and was leaving when he asked from behind me,

“Is the burden heavy?”

The moment I heard those words, emotion surged in my chest. It was the first time Father had spoken words containing comfort.

“It would be a lie to say it is as light as a feather, but it is not so heavy that I cannot endure it.”

“Then that is enough.”

Then that is enough. Why had those words been so pleasant to hear lately?

If someone recognizes my efforts… yes, then that is enough.

Chapter 96: There Was Nothing at the End of Your Life
-----------------------------------------------------
The next day, Utmost Evil Soma came to see me.

The sight of him slowly crossing the training ground of Hwangcheongak, wearing a white mask, created a truly overwhelming sense of pressure and terror.

The investigators and enforcement martial artists could not even whisper among themselves from afar. They all held their breath, bowed, and passed by. They feared him far more than they had when the Blood Heaven Blade Demon or the One-Flower Sword Venerable had come.

I stood by the window of my office and watched him.

Beside me, Seo Daeryong spoke in a trembling voice.

“Just what happened yesterday for that terrifying person to come to our pavilion?”

“Investigator Seo, the scariest place in our cult is Hwangcheongak. The person coming here should be the one trembling. Why are you trembling?”

“Yes, let’s say that’s true. Am I in a situation where I should evacuate or not? For the sake of our bond until now, please tell me. Please!”

Seeing that he still had the leisure to make jokes like this, he must have been learning martial arts well from the Blade Demon. Well, since he was putting his life into it, I expected he would soon show me a transformed self.

“It was only yesterday that you told me to keep worrying about you.”

“I’ll worry about you from afar, like yesterday.”

“Go and bring him in. My right arm should go.”

“I can only pray that Utmost Evil Soma doesn’t know I’m your right arm, Pavilion Lord.”

Seo Daeryong went outside like that, and my gaze returned to Utmost Evil Soma.

I knew he would come looking for me, but to think he would come this quickly, and in broad daylight, directly to Hwangcheongak.

Acting impulsively according to instinct was no different now than it had been back then. That was what made him an entertaining opponent, and that was what made him difficult to deal with.

A moment later, Seo Daeryong spoke from outside the door.

“The Demon Venerable of Utmost Evil has arrived.”

“Show him in.”

Soon after, Utmost Evil Soma entered, guided by Seo Daeryong.

“Welcome, elder.”

“You’re calling me elder again. I told you to treat me comfortably, like a friend.”

“I couldn’t possibly do that. Then I’ll call you Lord Soma.”

“Good.”

Utmost Evil Soma laughed aloud. As though nothing had happened yesterday, his behavior was completely natural.

When Seo Daeryong, who had shown him in, was about to leave, I quickly said,

“Ah, that fellow over there is Investigator Seo, my right arm.”

Seo Daeryong screamed with his expression, then, when Utmost Evil Soma looked back, greeted him with a calm face.

“I am Seo Daeryong. It is an honor to meet someone I respect.”

“Oh, I’ll remember you.”

“Thank you.”

Seo Daeryong walked out with an expression as though the world had ended. In his heart, he was probably screaming, Please don’t remember me! I laughed as I watched him.

Utmost Evil Soma abruptly asked,

“Isn’t it normal to hide someone you cherish?”

“If the other party is an enemy, yes. But you aren’t my enemy, Lord Soma, are you?”

“That doesn’t mean we’re friends yet, either.”

“If someone sets his mind on killing, would hiding ever keep anyone hidden, no matter how thoroughly? Lord Soma, have you ever failed to kill someone you intended to kill just because he hid?”

“No.”

“Exactly. Since life is uncertain and we never know when we’ll die anyway, shouldn’t he live properly as my right arm while he can?”

A peculiar light flowed through Utmost Evil Soma’s eyes.

“That is quite an interesting thing to say.”

Utmost Evil Soma rose from his seat and looked around the office. He looked over the piles of documents on the desk and the booklets inserted into the wall cabinet, then smelled the fragrance of the flowers in the pot by the window. It was the potted plant the One-Flower Sword Venerable had given me as a gift.

“The fragrance is pleasant.”

“Something recently made me come to like flowers.”

“The successor of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult liking flowers… If those bastards from the Murim Alliance come charging in, does that mean we’ll die in a flower field?”

Even at his mocking words, I responded calmly.

“Dying forlornly in some nameless field would have its own style, but isn’t this fine too? If the scent one smells in the final moment as breath leaves the body is not the smell of blood, but the fragrance of flowers.”

Instead of answering whether that was good or bad, Utmost Evil Soma burst into his habitual laughter.

“But what brings you to me?”

Utmost Evil Soma, who had been facing the flowers, turned back toward me.

“After the Second Young Master left yesterday, I thought about it carefully, and I found it deeply regrettable that we could not become friends.”

“I feel the same.”

“So I came to see if we might search for a way to become friends.”

“You came at the right time. So, did you find a way?”

“The method I thought of is for each of us to yield one thing.”

“How?”

“I will yield when it comes to the Second Young Master’s perspective on good and evil. I will try to understand the Second Young Master’s new demonic path.”

“Then what should I yield?”

“You said we could only become friends if I took off my mask. Can you yield on that?”

“Before that, let me ask you one thing. Why do you want to become friends with me?”

“I wish to get along with the Second Young Master without reserve.”

In truth, I knew why Utmost Evil Soma was doing this. First, he wanted to find out how I had killed the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable. When I returned to the cult, Utmost Evil Soma had been alive along with the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable.

What I felt then was this: the more evil one is, the longer one survives.

Utmost Evil Soma was also someone who valued his own life, so he would want to find out by any means how I had killed the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable. If the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable had been done in, then he would think he could be done in as well.

The other reason was his personality. He became fixated on those who provoked him and piqued his interest. He approached them, made them his friends, and when he grew bored, killed them immediately. It was his pathological taste that no one knew about, known only to me.

That was why I must not attach any good meaning whatsoever to his words about becoming friends. When he said he wanted to be friends, it meant he would play with you and then kill you.

These two reasons had made Utmost Evil Soma come personally all the way to my office. They were the reason he told the absurd lie that he would understand my demonic path.

“I want to become friends with the Second Young Master.”

Fine. In this life as well, I would become his friend.

Only when we became friends would he finally stand at a point where he was neither enemy nor ally.

That was exactly the kind of person Utmost Evil Soma was. A person who could barely escape being an enemy only after becoming a friend.

“Very well. Let’s each yield.”

At my answer, Utmost Evil Soma laughed loudly. To anyone listening, it was a hearty laugh of delight, but I was watching his eyes with the Divine Eye Art. He was looking at me with eyes that were endlessly cold.

Soma, at the end of your life, there was nothing.

He had said he had no regrets even in the moment of death, but that was a lie. The moment he recalled his childhood, he was already regretting it.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had changed, the One-Flower Sword Venerable had changed, and Cult Leader Pungcheon was changing too, but this Utmost Evil Soma would not change easily. He had been born with an evil nature, and from a young age, his master had brainwashed him as he raised him.

It was right then.

Seo Daeryong’s voice came from outside.

“The First Young Master has arrived.”

“Show him in.”

Utmost Evil Soma said with a smile,

“I called him.”

He came to see me and called my elder brother here? Truly, it was something only Utmost Evil Soma could do.

If the thought Shall I try this? occurred to him, he was the sort who would carry it out no matter what came afterward. Despite this impulsive personality, he had been one of the Demon Venerables who survived to the end, so one could tell just how extraordinary his malice was.

A moment later, Geom Muyang came inside. He greeted Utmost Evil Soma first.

“It has been a long time, Lord Soma.”

“Welcome, First Young Master. Now, I don’t need to introduce the two of you, do I?”

At his playful words, I played along.

“Perhaps we brothers are the very ones who should be introduced to each other. We know less about each other than you might think.”

When I treated Utmost Evil Soma comfortably, a slight tension crossed Geom Muyang’s face.

And then, taking it one step further.

Utmost Evil Soma said to Geom Muyang,

“I was in the middle of pestering the Second Young Master to become friends with me.”

Utmost Evil Soma observed Geom Muyang’s reaction with eyes that seemed to say, Let’s see how you respond.

My brother responded like my brother.

“At times, one does need such amusements.”

At Geom Muyang’s response, without the slightest disturbance, Utmost Evil Soma smiled and said,

“That is why I intend to enjoy my amusement with your younger brother. Our First Young Master is too upright, so he is no fun.”

“Mugeuk is overflowing with wit, so he will not be lacking as a friend.”

My brother had it hard too. He was not the type to come just because someone told him to, only to listen to words like that. He was enduring it all through sheer obsession with becoming the successor.

Yes, well done. Brother.

If you get on edge here, you lose in that instant. You’re doing well.

Whether he knew that feeling or not, Utmost Evil Soma provoked Geom Muyang.

“The Second Young Master came to see me yesterday. He asked me to support him instead of his elder brother.”

Even with me present, he did not hold back.

“Lord Soma, you said you wanted to be friends, so why are you driving a wedge between brothers? At this rate, we won’t be able to become good friends.”

At my words, Utmost Evil Soma giggled and said,

“There is nothing in this world as entertaining as sowing discord, so how do you expect me to hold back? I can hold in a shit, but I can’t hold that in.”

“If you’re going to sow discord, don’t do it to us. Do it to the Murim Alliance and the Unorthodox Alliance over there.”

“I would like to, but these days, I’m not in a position where I can.”

Geom Muyang simply listened to our conversation from the side. He was probably hoping I would make some verbal mistake that crossed the line.

“Since the two of you have gathered in one place like this, may I ask a question I have always been curious about?”

At Utmost Evil Soma’s question, my brother answered readily.

“Of course.”

“Then I will ask the First Young Master first. If I help make the First Young Master the successor, what will you do for me?”

As expected, Utmost Evil Soma was Utmost Evil Soma. To indulge such a vile hobby with the two of us in front of him.

Geom Muyang answered calmly, as though he had prepared for this.

“I will triple the support for the Valley of Villains and remove the restriction on the number of Faceless Guests. And I will lift the current prohibition forbidding Lord Soma from leaving the cult.”

Utmost Evil Soma was under a prohibition that forbade him from leaving the cult. The year before last, he had committed an incident in which he slaughtered martial artists of the Unorthodox Alliance, and it had nearly escalated into war.

“At the time, those bastards attacked first.”

That was how it began, but it ended in a massacre. After that, Father forbade him from going to the Central Plains for the time being. Nearly two years had already passed since then, so he must have been itching to death.

“Oh-ho, those are very attractive conditions.”

“Not at all. Aside from that, if there is anything Lord Soma desires, I will support it fully.”

Utmost Evil Soma laughed loudly. Did my brother know? That even as he laughed so happily, the gaze behind his mask had cooled to an endless chill.

“Now, this time I will ask the Second Young Master. If I help make the Second Young Master the Heavenly Demon, what will you do for me?”

Both of them were curious what my answer would be.

I gazed at Utmost Evil Soma and spoke quietly.

“I will let you live.”

For an instant, the atmosphere froze. The words I’m joking should have followed, but what I added was this.

“We talked yesterday, didn’t we? About what kind of new demonic path I aim for. And yet, what did you think I would do with you, Lord Soma, who upholds utmost evil? Did you think I would support you happily? Even if I must bear the political burden, I will let you live.”

My brother might have been inwardly cheering. Thinking that, in my youthful hot-bloodedness, I had ruined this excellent opportunity.

But there was something my brother did not know. Utmost Evil Soma was a man who became even more fascinated by an opponent when that opponent came at him like this.

As if proving that, the two eyes I saw through the open holes with the Divine Eye Art were shining brightly.

I spoke to him even more coldly than before.

“Suddenly, playing friends isn’t fun anymore, is it? Games that excessively break decorum never end well. So please do not ever again say you want to be friends.”

In the chilly atmosphere, Utmost Evil Soma rose from his seat.

“If we are not friends, then sitting together in the same place will only lead to a fight. Then I will see you next time.”

After bidding farewell to Geom Muyang as well, Utmost Evil Soma left the office.

When only the two of us remained, Geom Muyang asked me,

“What on earth are you thinking?”

My brother knew I was not someone who would be so rash as to anger Utmost Evil Soma carelessly.

“It just pisses me off, doesn’t it? You successors have to curry favor with us Eight Demon Venerables. Now, hurry up and wag your tail. Then I’ll seat you in the successor’s position. Doesn’t that piss you off, Brother?”

After hesitating for a moment over what to say, my brother rose from his seat and said,

“You are the one who always pisses me off.”

Leaving those words behind, Geom Muyang also left the office.

Soon after, Seo Daeryong came in.

“Were you waiting outside because you were worried about me?”

“Not about you, Pavilion Lord. I was worried he might have emitted demonic energy and made it wither.”

He looked at the potted flowers and was relieved.

I laughed and stood beside him.

Outside the building, Utmost Evil Soma could be seen walking away.

“He seemed angry. Will it be all right?”

“He wasn’t angry. Don’t you see that excited gait?”

“I’m not sure. It also looks like the gait of someone who wants to stomp someone to death.”

“If you’re that worried, put the flowerpot away in advance tomorrow.”

“I should. Pardon? Utmost Evil Soma is coming again tomorrow?”

Because Utmost Evil Soma was the kind of person who chased you to the end if you ran away after saying you did not want to do something.

“He is a person with whom one must settle things, one way or another.”

After a brief pause, Seo Daeryong asked,

“Pavilion Lord, how do you endure all this pressure?”

“I have a right arm, and I also have a left arm aiming for that right arm, and I have a heart, and wings, and now I even have a brain. What would be hard for me?”

I looked at Utmost Evil Soma’s back as he walked alone in the distance and added quietly,

“The one having a hard time is that wicked and lonely soul.”

Chapter 97: Why Are You Obsessed with My Mask?
----------------------------------------------
Geukak Soma came to find me again the next day.

“I thought you wouldn’t come anymore.”

“You hold the power of life and death over me. How could I not come?”

To Geukak Soma’s mockery of my words from yesterday about sparing him, I responded in my own way.

“Please wait a moment. It’s something I need to handle quickly.”

“Don’t mind me, take your time.”

I could have paused my work for a moment, but I deliberately made him wait.

Geukak Soma, who had walked over to the window, asked.

“I don’t see that flower from yesterday.”

I answered as I stamped a document.

“It seems Investigator Seo took it outside to water it for a moment.”

I reviewed the next document and stamped it again. And again. And again.

Geukak Soma, who had been looking out the window, must have grown tired of waiting, for he perched himself on my desk.

And with the air of someone thinking that since I had made him wait, he could endure this much rudeness, he looked down at me working.

“Why do you make work so difficult? Just kill them all.”

I said to him with a smile.

“Lord Soma, you live too easily.”

At my words, Geukak Soma let out a coarse laugh. You mustn’t be deceived by this unguarded appearance. Those who let their guard down against Soma all die; only those who remain tense in the face of Geukak survive.

“I’m finished.”

After stamping the last document and raising my head, Geukak Soma spoke unexpected words.

“If you truly become the successor, will you spare me?”

Of course, I mustn’t be taken in by these words either. As if he would come begging for his life?

He is enjoying this entire process. Geukak Soma’s wish is to interact like this and someday see me crumble. He is the type of person who must cackle and mock me at that moment to feel satisfied.

I took out a dagger and drew a long line across the table.

“I will spare you. But…”

From the center, I drew a vertical line slightly to the right.

“You have to come this far to survive.”

Soma’s eyes flashed with interest.

“What is this?”

“It is the measure of how much I care.”

“Oho! Something like this?”

“You have to cross the halfway point. A friend for whom you feel no affection whatsoever is no different from a stranger, is he? A friend in name alone dies.”

“Surviving is difficult.”

“You’ll have to find a way across somehow.”

“Where do I stand now?”

I drew a vertical line at a spot just past the starting point.

“Only this far?”

“You’re already here.”

Geukak Soma looked down at the line, then back at me, then down at the line again.

He couldn’t help but fall into this relationship.

Not only did I treat him casually, but he must also sense from me a flavor of negotiation and compromise that could never be found in the main cult. That would become the most stimulating spice for Geukak Soma.

“What does the Second Young Master think of the Cult Leader?”

The reason he asks is probably to confirm whether I’m doing this because I trust my father, or whether I have received secret orders from him.

“I don’t think about my father. I just try to feel things as they come. Father isn’t someone I can understand with my head. It’s only exhausting for no reason.”

As if my words resonated with him, Geukak Soma laughed.

“If you’re finished speaking, let’s get up. It’s mealtime.”

“You must eat. Let’s go out.”

I left the office with Geukak Soma. As we exited the building, he followed right after me.

“Where will you be dining?”

“I thought I’d go out to Maga Village for a meal today.”

“Let us go together.”

I asked him, feigning surprise.

“Are you sure that’s alright?”

Since he was someone who never removed his mask, I had never heard of him dining with anyone.

“It’s fine. I will simply sit while the Second Young Master eats.”

“I wasn’t asking about you, Lord Soma. I meant Maga Village. Is that alright?”

Only then did Geukak Soma realize it was a joke telling him not to run amok or commit slaughter, and he burst into loud laughter.

While Seo Dae-ryong, hugging a flower pot to his chest, watched us with concern from across the way, we cut straight across the training grounds and headed outside.

* * *

Just before entering Maga Village, Geukak Soma spoke.

“Did you know? I’ve never come out to Maga Village. When I had business outside, I would just pass through; this is my first time walking here.”

“Isn’t that fortunate for the villagers of Maga?”

He laughed at my joke.

“Is it alright for me to walk these streets?”

“If you’re worried that people will be startled and panic, you needn’t concern yourself with that.”

“Why?”

“You’ll see.”

And so we entered Maga Village. Even as we walked down the street, everyone passed by without much fuss. A few people who recognized me bowed their heads in greeting, but no one looked at Geukak Soma beside me and recoiled in terror.

“Why is no one afraid of me?”

“Because no one thinks you are Geukak Soma.”

“Shouldn’t they know it’s me when they see this mask?”

“Look over there.”

I pointed to a stall across the street. The stand was selling all manner of items related to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“Your mask is over there too, Lord Soma.”

“!”

Geukak Soma was startled.

Masks worn by the Faceless Agents were actually hanging there. Not just white masks, but even ones painted with colors or decorated with drawings.

“Why these…!”

As Geukak Soma raised his hand as if to blow them all away, I tugged his sleeve.

“I believe that hand will be needed over there.”

Just then, a child holding his father’s hand and wearing a Faceless Agent’s mask caught sight of Geukak Soma wearing the same mask and waved eagerly.

“It doesn’t cost anything. Please wave back.”

When I gently shook his sleeve, Geukak Soma reluctantly waved his hand.

Without knowing that he might be the only person Geukak Soma had ever waved to, the child passed by us.

“Don’t be too surprised. That, too, is possible.”

The stall next to it was selling wooden dolls; not only dolls modeled after the Eight Demon Lords, but also dolls modeled after the Heavenly Demon.

Geukak Soma was truly astonished to see the Heavenly Demon dolls.

“Is this truly allowed?”

“If they don’t sell such things in a village near the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, where would they sell them? They couldn’t sell them in front of the Martial Alliance, could they?”

“Rather, they could probably sell them in front of the Martial Alliance. It’s here that they couldn’t.”

“It’s thanks to Father’s magnanimity. He permitted them to make and sell as much as they wished. Since that day, goods related to Father and the Demon Lords have flooded out. You’d think it would be too uncomfortable to make them, but the power of profit is a mighty law indeed.”

“This is truly amusing.”

Only now did Geukak Soma laugh heartily. Passersby glanced at him, but no one thought he was Geukak Soma and simply moved on.

“Lord Soma, when people like us come out here, it feels like we should naturally be the protagonists. But this is a world where those people are the protagonists. If they recognize us, they may fear us and bow their heads, but what is important to them isn’t us—it is their own lives. They must earn money to survive each day, meet people, and raise children. Maga Village is such a place.”

“Fascinating. How does the Second Young Master know this so well?”

“From a young age, I would secretly leave the cult and wander Maga Village. This place was my world.”

I glossed over it to him like that. He wouldn’t have believed me if I said I had traveled the wide world, but it sounded plausible enough for Maga Village.

Geukak Soma’s gaze turned again to the stalls selling masks and Heavenly Demon dolls.

Without hesitation, he unleashed his unique demonic art, the Lesser Evil Heart. The merchants selling goods frowned, feeling a tightness in their chests, and rubbed themselves.

“Soon that one will put on the mask he was selling and stab the person next to him to death. If they die, they’ll die because of the Second Young Master. Because you are the one who brought me here.”

“Don’t be unreasonable. You are the one who followed me to eat. And you would be the one killing them. Furthermore, there is one thing you must know.”

“What is that?”

“Committing murder before the Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion is punishable by execution. Try swapping them—those people’s lives and Lord Soma’s life.”

I started walking first and added,

“It’s fine to touch those Father dolls over there, but you must not touch the livelihoods of people trying to make a living.”

I felt Geukak Soma withdraw his Lesser Evil Heart behind me. He had done it to test my reaction, not because he intended to kill them from the start.

He finds playing with me amusing right now. The more he yields, the more he must be anticipating the moment he kills me, expecting an even more thrilling pleasure.

The place I arrived at was a tavern.

“Here, this is my regular spot.”

“It’s shabby. Why here of all places?”

“It is a bit tiresome since I come all the time, but I like the owner here. He is the only person who knows you are the real Lord Soma.”

That man, Jo Chun-bae, came out and greeted me warmly as always.

“Welcome, Pavilion Lord.”

His gaze turned toward Geukak Soma.

For a moment, Jo Chun-bae flinched and showed a fearful expression.

“Truly, only this man knows who I am.”

“It is because he came with me. He knows the real one has arrived.”

I said to Jo Chun-bae with a smile.

“It is indeed the person you are thinking of. Fortunately, I am the only one eating, so you needn’t worry too much.”

“Yes!”

Nevertheless, the pressure Geukak Soma gave off seemed greater than even Father’s. According to rumor, Geukak Soma was a villain who killed people for no reason.

“I think he fears you more than any guest I have ever served. More than my father, even.”

“The Cult Leader came too?”

“Yes.”

“Unexpected.”

“Everyone has a first time.”

“Everyone has that first time with the Second Young Master.”

I smiled faintly, and he habitually followed with a laugh.

This time, I asked.

“Have you ever eaten at a tavern like this?”

“I did when I was young.”

“With whom?”

He didn’t answer.

But I knew who it was. Before becoming Soma’s disciple, he had said he once ate at a street stall with my father. It was a single memory that remained in his mind like a painting.

I suddenly tried to imagine that scene. A little child entering the tavern holding my father’s hand.

Geukak Soma said he was born drenched in killing from a young age, but was that really true? Would that little child’s eyes, upon entering the tavern, really have been filled with killing intent?

Meanwhile, the meal arrived.

Perhaps feeling that a single mistake could truly get him killed, Jo Chun-bae was careful even to the point of breathing cautiously.

The look in his eyes as he safely set down the dishes and retreated seemed to say, “I have achieved great success as a tavern owner today thanks to this.”

I replied with my eyes. That remains to be seen, Owner. Who knows what kind of occasion will arise next.

I picked up my chopsticks and asked Geukak Soma out of courtesy.

“Will you eat as well?”

“Is there a way to eat when this mask has no mouth hole?”

“How about slightly lifting the mask and eating from underneath?”

“Wouldn’t that make me look ridiculous?”

“Then there is no way.”

“I’m not hungry, so don’t mind me and eat.”

“Yes, then I’ll eat first.”

I truly ate alone. Geukak Soma merely watched me. Anyone looking would call it madness, but I was accomplishing the difficult feat of eating alone in front of Geukak Soma.

“Isn’t it hard to keep wearing the mask?”

“Wouldn’t it be harder for the Second Young Master than for me?”

“Me?”

“Since you have no mask, you must put on various expressions throughout the day, no?”

“Ah, that may indeed be the case.”

Starting with that story, I told him many things.

I told him tales of how Hyeolcheon Doma, Ilhwa Sword Lord, and Mabul came here, stories about the weather, and that white masks would probably sell well in front of the Martial Alliance. He listened to my stories silently.

As I finished my meal and set down my chopsticks, Geukak Soma spoke unexpected words.

“Second Young Master, I have a request.”

“Please speak.”

“Please speak to the Cult Leader and have my prohibition seal removed.”

I made a startled expression. But when he said “request,” I had predicted he would ask this. Originally, this was a request made to my older brother, but now that his fate and mine have changed, he is making this request of me.

“It is no easy matter.”

“That is why I ask the Second Young Master. Somehow, I feel that if it is you, it might be resolved.”

“What will you do for me if I have the prohibition seal removed?”

“Just say the word. What do you wish? Do you want my full support?”

My brother had wanted that. But I was drawing a bigger picture.

“No.”

“You don’t want my support?”

“I don’t want forced support. It would only break the unity of those who support me.”

“Then what do you want?”

Staring into the two eyes behind the mask, I spoke.

“When it is just the two of us alone, please remove your mask.”

“!”

The atmosphere froze instantly.

“Why are you so obsessed with my mask?”

“I am not obsessed with the mask.”

“Then what?”

“Since we are becoming friends anyway, I wish to be true friends.”

“!”

Geukak Soma, silent for a moment, asked coldly.

“Can you handle me without the mask?”

Gazing into his two eyes, shining with primal evil intent through the gaping holes, I calmly returned the question.

“Without that mask, can you handle me?”

Chapter 98: My World Will Be Boring.
------------------------------------
Within the eye holes, Geukak Soma's eyes were smiling wide.

Had he ever imagined that I would demand he remove his mask as the price for undoing his restriction?

He was furious, yet at the same time he was enjoying himself. I was likely the first opponent to make two such conflicting emotions coexist inside him.

He had probably never in his life met anyone who treated him so recklessly. Even Father had never treated the demons so casually.

I know. I know how much Geukak Soma enjoys moments like this. His blood would be racing and his heart pounding madly. I consider that a kind of illness too.

“What if I promise to remove my mask and then fail to keep my word?”

“Then I’ll have to rush in and force it off.”

“Well then, try practicing right now.”

In an instant, Geukak Soma's gaze sank. Having readied himself for a fight, he had become an entirely different person from moments before.

I waved my hands and pushed my chair back.

“If that happened, the skin on my face might get peeled off.”

Geukak Soma relaxed his posture and asked leisurely.

“Well, if you cannot force it off either, what will you do now?”

I stared at him briefly, then answered calmly.

“I won’t do anything.”

For a moment, Geukak Soma flinched. I had just spoken the correct answer that even Geukak Soma himself did not know.

Doing nothing.

For this contrarian Geukak Soma, this was the answer. With an expression showing he could not understand, he asked me.

“Does this mean you trust me?”

“Honestly, I do not trust you. Geukak Soma is not exactly a name that inspires trust, is it?”

I smiled as if joking, but he did not laugh.

“Now, let us get up. You should go eat as well, Lord Soma.”

I did not explain my reasoning to him. Sometimes, not speaking produces the greatest effect. That is especially true when there is no particular reason, as was the case now.

Sometimes, the other person will inflate this trivial matter and craft some plausible reason out of it on their own.

I rose from my seat first, and Geukak Soma followed.

Leaving the pleasure house, we retraced our steps.

He, who had been walking beside me for a moment, halted.

“Please wait a moment.”

He went over to a peddler selling masks. There, he bought a white mask and gave it to me.

“A gift to commemorate today.”

Accepting the mask, I spoke to him.

“I demanded that you remove your mask, yet here you are putting one on my face instead.”

I obediently put on the mask.

“It is suffocating. I assure you, if you fought without a mask, Lord Soma, you would fight twice as well. Just how long must you spend wearing this before it becomes comfortable?”

“It does not take as long as you would think.”

“If that is so, the opposite must also be true. I mean the time it takes for your mind to grow comfortable without one.”

Through the eye holes of the mask, Geukak Soma was silently gazing at me.

So this is how you have been seeing me.

Now he too could observe me only through my eyes within the eye holes. Perhaps that would be even more disadvantageous for me. Geukak Soma had lived an eternity among countless subordinates who wore masks.

I pulled the mask up onto my head like a hat.

“Wear it like this. How comfortable and pleasant is that? Put it on when you want, take it off when you want. Exactly like this.”

As I spoke, I tapped the mask that was looking up at the sky atop my head with my palm.

“This fellow must feel good too. Looking up at the sky like this.”

At that, Geukak Soma looked up at the sky.

“Would this not suffice? Then both of us can see the sky.”

After gazing at the sky with empty eyes for some time, when Geukak Soma turned his gaze back to me, he had already made his decision.

“Very well. I accept the condition proposed by the Second Young Master. If you release my restriction, I shall remove my mask when we are alone.”

I did not believe he would keep his promise.

Before my regression, I had seen his face only once, just before death. He would not easily remove his mask.

But the mere fact that he made that promise held meaning. Would I truly get to see his face from his youth?

“Thank you, Lord Soma.”

The vertical line on the line drawn in my heart had shifted slightly further to the right. It was still far. Only after it had crossed more than halfway would he reach the state of contemplating whether or not to kill me.

“I suppose I need at least this much leverage to stand up to Father, do I not?”

We looked at each other and smiled. Neither of us invested any sincerity in it, yet as if this laughter were the seal of our promise, we smiled broadly.

* * *

The next day, when I entered Cheonma Hall, Chief Strategist Sima Ming was also present.

After greeting Father, I bantered with Sima Ming.

“These days, I have been driven into a corner because of you, Strategist.”

“What do you mean?”

“Father has been on my case about those two points.”

Only then did Sima Ming realize I was speaking of Go, and he laughed.

“It is because you were swindled by a strategist who cannot play Go.”

“So you had already heard.”

This time, I laughed. Father was usually so taciturn that one would not expect him to share such jokes, yet he had told Sima Ming everything. It meant Sima Ming was that close to him.

After exchanging such light greetings, I revealed today's purpose to Father.

“Father, I have come to ask a favor.”

“Speak.”

“Please release Geukak Soma's restriction.”

Father was not surprised. Perhaps it was because he had been briefed on my recent meetings with Geukak Soma.

Sima Ming stepped in on behalf of Father, who was silently looking down at me.

“May I ask the reason?”

“Geukak Soma came and asked me.”

“Then could you not have refused?”

“If I refused and he threatened to kill your family, what then? Ah, then I suppose I would become the Cheonma. Should I have accepted?”

Sima Ming did not laugh at my joke.

“This is no matter for jokes. Do you know why Geukak Soma was placed under restriction?”

“I do. He massacred the martial artists of the Xiedao Meng.”

“Soma is a man who cannot control his anger.”

“The longer he is bound, the greater his anger will grow until it explodes.”

Sima Ming could not refute this.

Father, who had been listening, suddenly spoke.

“Can you take responsibility?”

“Why should I take responsibility? The one who causes the trouble should bear it.”

“If a dog off its leash bites someone, how can only the dog be responsible? The one who unleashed it must bear responsibility as well.”

“That may be true, but…”

“If you accept the condition of taking responsibility, I will release the restriction.”

“Surely I cannot look after him forever? Once the restriction is lifted, he will leave the sect immediately. I will take responsibility only for what happens during that departure.”

“Very well!”

Unlike Father, who readily agreed, Sima Ming emphasized again with an uneasy expression.

“Geukak Soma has accumulated much during the two years he was confined to the main sect. Should he go out to the Central Plains, he may commit slaughter.”

“I will keep that in mind and watch him carefully.”

Finally, I expressed my gratitude to Father.

“Father, thank you for allowing this.”

Of course, Father did not readily accept my thanks.

“Is this truly something to be thankful for?”

From Cheonma Hall's standpoint, there was a limit to how long Geukak Soma could be kept under restriction. My request came at a time when it provided Father with sufficient cause to release the restriction. Consequently, this matter had become yet another trial for the successor.

Father. I am sorry, but I have the advantage this time. I already know what Geukak Soma is planning to do after leaving the sect.

* * *

After leaving Cheonma Hall, I went straight to Akingok to find Geukak Soma.

“Did you truly receive permission?”

Geukak Soma seemed not to have expected me to obtain Father's permission in a single day.

“Yes. The restriction will be lifted tomorrow. An official document will arrive from Tongcheon Pavilion by tomorrow.”

Admiration flashed in Geukak Soma's eyes.

“I did not think you would accomplish it so quickly.”

“Now do you understand why you should support me rather than my brother?”

“I shall think on it seriously. But did the Sect Leader truly grant permission just like that?”

“As if that would happen. Father set a condition.”

“What condition?”

I let out a rather exaggerated sigh before speaking to him.

“If you cause any problems, Lord Soma, I am to take full responsibility for them.”

“Oho! You have wagered your position as successor for my sake.”

“Something like that.”

Geukak Soma could not hide his astonishment.

“Is this worth that much?”

“Seeing Lord Soma's face? I believe it is more than worth it.”

“To think you would wager so much for mere flattery, Second Young Master.”

“Then since the flattery has already been exposed, allow me one more. Becoming friends with Geukak Soma is something no one else in the world will ever experience. It is a condition well worth wagering on.”

Geukak Soma stared at me piercingly. He probably wanted to discern my true intentions, but he would not be able to know me through my eyes and expression alone. Not in the past, not now, and not in the future.

“In other words, I can cause all the trouble I wish. The Second Young Master will take responsibility, after all.”

“It is sheer madness.”

“Why did you not hide it? You did not have to tell me you promised to take responsibility. This could have been a weakness for the Second Young Master.”

“I had to tell you. Are not most of the problems we face caused by failing to speak when we should?”

Geukak Soma smiled strangely. It was a smile different from his habitual one.

“I have no intention of slighting you, Lord Soma. Nor do I believe that hiding things will prevent them from happening.”

“Whenever I heard rumors of the Second Young Master, I thought they were exaggerated. Yet now I see they were greatly downplayed.”

“Perhaps that is because of you, Lord Soma.”

“What do you mean?”

“Depending on one's emotions, even the same report can sound different. However, before you, subordinates cannot help but stiffen. They would deliver only dry reports stripped of personal opinion and emotion. They must have failed to convey what I am truly like.”

“A very reasonable point.”

The laughter sounded different again. Soma laughs like that when he has much on his mind.

“Instead, please promise me one thing.”

“What is it?”

“If you are to leave the sect, you must go with me. Should you break this promise, the restriction will be reimposed, and the period will likely start anew from the beginning.”

“Let us not wait for later—let us leave the sect tomorrow at once.”

This was Geukak Soma, a man who made such impulsive decisions. That was why dealing with him was difficult.

I answered without hesitation as well.

“I look forward to our journey, Lord Soma.”

I deliberately phrased his departure from the sect as a journey.

“We are not going on a journey.”

“If you go with a friend, is not anywhere a journey?”

I did not want to approach this journey with a heart anxious about preventing him from causing incidents.

I would go out with the mindset of truly taking a brief excursion with Geukak Soma. If a friend fights, stop him; if a friend falls into crisis, save him. With exactly that mindset.

“Then I shall take my leave.”

“I will see you tomorrow morning, Second Young Master.”

As I opened the door to leave, Geukak Soma asked from behind.

“Is there anywhere you wish to go?”

I turned back to him and said.

“Many places. But for this departure, I wish to see Lord Soma's world. Please show me around.”

“My world will be boring. Perhaps even more so than Magachon.”

“It only feels that way because it is your world. To another, it will be different.”

He smiled, and I smiled with him.

Meeting Soma makes me smile often. Whether it is a forced smile, a dagger hidden within laughter, or a sincere one. Either way, I end up smiling. They say those who smile often live long. So there is this benefit as well.

As I opened the door to leave again, I added one more thing.

“By the way, Magachon is not such a boring place. After all, it is full of people who make their living selling masks to Geukak Soma.”

Chapter 99: When I Return After Completing This Journey.
--------------------------------------------------------
After leaving the Valley of Villains, I went to the Southern Blade Sect, where the Blood Heaven Blade Demon resided.

Seo Daeryong, who had been training in the courtyard there, was startled out of his wits when he saw the white mask dangling from my waist.

“Gasp! Don’t tell me—did you kill the Extreme Evil Young Demon too?”

As if he himself was shocked by what he had just said, Seo Daeryong hurriedly clapped a hand over his mouth.

“Of course. Did you think I’d let a villain like that live?”

When I spoke coldly, Seo Daeryong instead lowered the hand covering his mouth in relief.

“Thank goodness. I really thought you’d killed him.”

“I said I killed him!”

“You’re not foolish enough to go around wearing evidence of murder at your waist, Pavilion Lord.”

“If you know that, why ask if I killed him?”

“I was so shocked the moment I saw the mask. But what is that mask? Are you thinking of infiltrating the Valley of Villains?”

“Why would I need this to infiltrate that place? You’d need it if you were infiltrating.”

My Four Steps of the Wind God had now risen to a level where no Faceless Guest could sense my presence.

“This is a gift the Extreme Evil Young Demon gave me.”

Seo Daeryong was even more shocked than before.

“The Extreme Evil Young Demon gave you a gift?”

“And it was a gift the Young Demon personally bought for me in Maga Village, even paying money for it.”

“It’s more surprising that a man like that carries money. He seems like the sort who’d just kill someone and take what he wants. Anyway, Pavilion Lord, you’ll be the first and last person ever to receive a gift from the Extreme Evil Young Demon.”

“Investigator Seo.”

“Yes.”

“What we know least, apart from what lies inside a person’s heart, is our own future. On the day we first met, could you ever have imagined that you and I would be having this conversation in the courtyard of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s home?”

“Well, that’s true. Back when I fell for your coaxing at the tavern, I really had no idea this training hell would be waiting for me.”

Watching him sigh, I smiled.

“One day, you’ll say this too. I truly had no idea that hellish training back then would save my life.”

Seo Daeryong looked at me and grinned. Seo Daeryong himself was living proof of those words. There was no crookedness or gloom to be seen in him now. One never knew what lay ahead for a person.

Leaving him behind as he began training again, I entered the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s house.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was taking books down from the shelf.

“Keep playing tricks like that and you’ll lose your head.”

“How can such a refined man who reads poetry say something so vulgar?”

“It’s because you’re wearing that damned unlucky thing.”

I had put on the white mask as a prank when I entered the room.

As I had done with the Extreme Evil Young Demon, I pushed the mask up onto my head like a hat.

“I’ll be leaving the cult tomorrow with the Extreme Evil Young Demon. I came to ask you to look after matters inside the cult while I’m away.”

Still pulling books from the shelf, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon spoke without turning around.

“No need to worry. Things will run just fine on their own. They run without me, and they’ll run perfectly well without you too.”

“Right? They run too well, and that’s the problem. Oh, and please tell the Sword Venerable in my stead, Elder.”

“Me?”

“Yes. Tell him I left without paying my respects because I was busy.”

The reason I deliberately asked the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was because I hoped the two of them would meet one more time under the pretext of this errand.

Perhaps the Blood Heaven Blade Demon guessed my intention as well, for he did not bother refusing. At times like this, it was clear he too wished to reconcile with the Sword Venerable.

“Then I’ll be off.”

I was about to turn and leave when the Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked from behind me.

“Do you truly intend to draw the Extreme Evil Young Demon to our side?”

“Even if he doesn’t become our ally, I at least won’t let him become our enemy.”

“Is there any need to go that far?”

“If the Extreme Evil Young Demon joins hands with my elder brother, he’ll become several times more troublesome than he is now.”

“In any case, the Extreme Evil Young Demon isn’t the sort of man who would sincerely serve the First Young Master.”

“That’s the problem. The two of them will keep a suitable distance while exchanging tasks and rewards as they move forward. Given their personalities, they won’t have any emotional clashes, nor will either of them have his pride wounded. That’s why they’ll get along even better than with the Demon Buddha.”

Because that was exactly what had happened in reality. The two had worked so well together that, in the end, they became closer than the Demon Buddha or the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Then I’ll be going.”

“Wait a moment.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon put the books he had taken out back into places other than where they had originally been. The moment the final book slid into place—

Srrrk.

The bookshelf opened, revealing a secret room. It was an intricate secret passage that opened when specific books were placed in specific spots.

More surprising than the fact that there was a secret room here was that he had opened it without hesitation in front of me.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon entered the secret room and came back out carrying something.

He casually tossed what he had brought onto the table in front of me.

“Take it.”

“What is this?”

When I opened the neatly wrapped package, there was a thin upper garment inside.

“It’s the Ghost-Guard Garment. If you wear it, it’ll hurt a little less when you get beaten.”

It was not merely a matter of hurting a little less. The Ghost-Guard Garment was a top-grade protective garment, impervious to blades and swords.

“Are you truly giving me something this precious?”

“I am.”

“You should wear it yourself, Elder.”

“What would an old man like me do with it? It’d be unsightly.”

“Then sell it.”

“And after selling it?”

“You’d make money.”

“And after making it?”

Come to think of it, he had no blood relatives to pass his wealth on to.

“Travel through the Central Plains and eat delicacies from mountains and seas.”

“Delicacies, my foot. Now that I’m old, even the noodles I eat at lunch don’t digest properly.”

My heart ached because I knew it was not entirely a joke. I had experienced it myself, so I knew. No matter how great a master one was, aging could not be helped.

“You don’t want to wear it? If you don’t, then forget it.”

“Of course that’s not true.”

When he tried to take it back, I swiftly snatched it up and put on the Ghost-Guard Garment right there. Wasn’t it only proper etiquette to wear such a protective garment on the spot?

“It fits perfectly! It’s really comfortable.”

It was thin and elastic, so it felt as if I were not wearing it at all. If I wrapped the Supreme Heavenly Silkworm Thread over this and activated the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art, my body would not be cut apart even if I were struck by sword qi or sword force.

Far removed from showing off his generosity, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had already gone out into the courtyard where Seo Daeryong was, as if worried I might thank him, and had begun nagging.

After watching the two of them train for a short while, I quietly left his residence.

I did not intend to repay the Blood Heaven Blade Demon with words.

* * *

“When did you say you were leaving?”

For some reason, Ian was even less worried about me than the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had been.

“Tomorrow morning.”

“Then we can’t hold a farewell party. Ah, and I thought we might finally all gather and have a drink after so long.”

“Ian! Is that what matters right now? I’m going out to the Central Plains with the Extreme Evil Young Demon. ‘Won’t that be dangerous? I’ll guard you!’ Where did she go? My old Ian!”

“I miss her too, that affectionate Ian.”

“This is all because of training. I’ll have to stop you from doing it.”

I approached and examined Ian’s hands. The layers of calluses on her palms spoke of the harsh training she had undergone.

“These days, when I grip a sword, something feels different.”

There was no further explanation, but I knew what she meant.

The Flying Heaven Sword Art, which had reached the eighth stage, must have been ceaselessly tempting and provoking her. Urging her to meet strong opponents and fight.

Compared to me or the Demon Venerables, she was still lacking in many ways, but the Flying Heaven Sword Art at the eighth stage was a level of skill where, once she entered the martial world, she would be hard-pressed to find a worthy opponent.

“I’ll go and tremble in fear beside the terrifying Extreme Evil Young Demon.”

“I heard you went to the Heavenly Demon Hall yourself to receive permission so you could suffer that terrifying ordeal.”

“Who did you hear that from?”

“Investigator Seo has already been here.”

“He must be the loosest-lipped right-hand man in the martial world.”

Ian did not laugh at my joke.

“I was always the first to know news about you, Young Master. And whenever you left the cult like this, I naturally followed you.”

“Are you upset?”

At that, her true feelings burst out.

“Yes, I’m upset. I’m very, very upset.”

With a long sigh, the emotions she had been holding back poured out.

“I’m worried! I want to follow you and protect you! I’m no longer your guard, and you’ve even given me the excessive position of captain of the Ghost Shadow Unit, but what I still want is to stay by your side and protect you. I’m someone who wants to die beside you, even if I die. That is my dream… I thought that if I became a greater master than I am now, I’d be able to truly protect you, that I should endure until I became someone useful like that, that I should endure and keep training…”

Then Ian choked up.

“…I know you’re so busy and having such a hard time… so I didn’t want to say this… I’m sorry. I’ll keep training hard.”

In the end, the tears she had been holding back fell from her eyes.

“Pack your things.”

For a moment, Ian flinched in surprise.

“I’m sorry. I was wrong. Please don’t drive me away.”

“What are you talking about? Pack your things. Let’s go together tomorrow.”

In that instant, she was startled.

“Really?”

“You’re right. If you don’t protect me, who will? Let’s go together. Go and protect me to your heart’s content.”

“Good! That’s really great!”

For the first time in a long while, Ian was so happy that she jumped up and down.

“Thank you, Young Master. Thank you so much.”

“Once you leave home, all that awaits is hardship. What are you thanking me for? And our companion is the Extreme Evil Young Demon, no less.”

“I’d go even if it were the Cult Leader, not the Extreme Evil Young Demon! I’m going, I tell you! I’ll see you later. I have a lot to prepare. Women have many things to get ready!”

As if she were busy, Ian ran out of the training ground first.

Watching her so excited, I realized something.

Martial arts training was good, and changes in life were good as well, but the most important thing was that emotion she was now leaping about with in joy… this moment, right now, was the most important.

Perhaps, behind my wish for her to become stronger, there had been selfishness meant to put my own heart at ease.

Perhaps a cowardly unconscious thought had been at work in me, thinking that her hardships would soon become mine.

Her life was so vividly alive, unfolding in the present, and yet I might have driven her into this training ground to conceal my own anxiety.

I finally realized that the true time to perform the Divine Poison Purification Art had nothing to do with her—it was the moment I overcame my own anxiety. That was why I was able to make my decision.

Yes, now was truly the time.

By the time she returned from this journey, she would return as a different person.

* * *

The next morning, I went to the Valley of Villains with Ian.

When we arrived, the Extreme Evil Young Demon had already finished preparing to leave.

“I intend to go quietly, so I’ll only bring one friend to attend to me.”

The mask of the Faceless Guest introduced by the Extreme Evil Young Demon had a stylish blue line drawn across it.

“This is Blue Mask.”

He greeted me respectfully. As the Extreme Evil Young Demon’s right-hand man, he was someone I knew well.

His martial arts were excellent, and his loyalty was even more excellent. When I had come to search for the Great Art of Regression, he had still been the Extreme Evil Young Demon’s right-hand man.

And he was also an ill-fated man who could not become the next Extreme Evil Young Demon because the current one lived too long.

I introduced Ian to them as well.

“This is my guard.”

Ian greeted the Extreme Evil Young Demon politely.

“I am Ian. It is an honor to meet you.”

I added one more word.

“She is also my heart.”

Ian’s face turned slightly red, but because of the occasion, she simply kept her head bowed in silence.

At my words, the Extreme Evil Young Demon smiled and said,

“First you reveal your right arm, and now you tell me where your heart is.”

“I’m exposing all my weaknesses so thoroughly that no one will know which one is the real weakness.”

“Well then, shall we depart?”

“Good.”

The Extreme Evil Young Demon and I climbed into the carriage, while Ian and Blue Mask sat on the driver’s bench. The two of them exchanged brief nods from the bench. Though they had only just met, they shared the common bond of guarding their masters.

Once the carriage set off, the Extreme Evil Young Demon did not hide his delight.

“I feel good.”

“It has been a long time since I’ve gone to the Central Plains, so I feel good as well.”

I meant it. For quite some time, I had been confined to the cult, going only between the Yellow Springs Pavilion, the training ground, the Heavenly Demon Hall, and the Pungryu Tavern.

At least, whenever I felt stifled, I had been able to use the Spacetime Transposition Art to rest in new places; had it not been for that, I would truly have felt suffocated. So there was no need to even mention the suffocation the Extreme Evil Young Demon must have felt after being confined for two years.

“You brought that mask.”

The white mask he had given me as a gift was hanging from my waist.

“You gave it to me, Young Demon. Of course I had to bring it.”

“I’ll have a better one made for you, not something cheap like that.”

He meant that he would have one made like the one he wore, where the eyes could not be seen from the outside.

“No, it’s all right. I prefer this one.”

“You prefer that?”

“Did you not buy it for me yourself, Young Demon? That alone is enough.”

“Second Young Master. I am someone who begins by suspecting people when I look at them. The more pleasing their words, the more I suspect them.”

“Of course you should suspect me. I’m treating someone with ‘Extreme Evil’ in his title this well, so surely I must have some ulterior motive.”

“What is your ulterior motive, Second Young Master?”

I grinned.

“You asked whether making all sorts of expressions was not more tiring, didn’t you? I actually prefer it. At times like this, I can use an ambiguous expression to gloss over my answer.”

He could not press me too closely. After all, I was not urging him to keep his promise to remove his mask either.

“Why do you not ask where we are going?”

“We must be going somewhere in this dreary world.”

At my joke, the Extreme Evil Young Demon laughed loudly.

Meanwhile, the carriage left the main cult and began picking up speed toward the Central Plains.

Chapter 100: The Jianghu Never Sleeps.
--------------------------------------
The carriage continued racing toward somewhere.

I was curious. Where in the world would Geugak Soma go first?

I knew about the major incident that had occurred when he left the sect this time, but I did not know the finer details—where he would go first, whom he would meet, and such.

There must have been someone who had offended him during the past two years. Was he going to kill that someone?

Or would he rent out an entire brothel and hold a debauched feast?

Perhaps he might kill someone and then go to a brothel.

Then Geugak Soma abruptly asked.

“Why did that guard become a heart?”

He seemed like someone who wouldn’t ask about a mere guard, but he showed interest because it was someone I liked.

“My guard became my heart because she is alive.”

“Then is the Second Young Master dead right now?”

I stared at him and asked.

“Are we alive?”

To Geugak Soma, who was bewildered about how to answer for a moment, I added with a smile.

“She is also pretty.”

“You have peculiar tastes.”

“In my eyes, she is the prettiest.”

“Before returning to the cult, I shall have to elevate our Second Young Master’s eye for women.”

“Perhaps I will be the one to show you.”

I wished Seo Daeryong were here.

Inspector Seo, who would have imagined you would end up discussing the eye for women with Geugak Soma? The future is a strange thing.

Then, Cheongmyeon’s voice was heard from the driver’s seat.

“We will stop for a meal in the village.”

From the first day of the journey, we had eaten in separate pairs.

Sometimes at different inns, and sometimes Geugak Soma would rent a separate room to eat.

Today too, after naturally finishing a meal with I An and stepping out of the inn, I saw a group of dark-path thugs harassing a merchant down at the end of the road.

I was just pretending not to see and turning to walk toward the carriage when someone spoke from behind.

“You are just pretending not to see and leaving.”

It was Geugak Soma, who had just finished his meal at the inn across the street.

“So it was merely a show of justice to become the successor, after all?”

I smiled at his mockery and said.

“I do have a somewhat pretentious side.”

“I quite like it. That is how a person should be. Pretending to be good is a headache.”

We looked at each other and laughed.

As we walked toward the carriage, Geugak Soma asked.

“Why does the Second Young Master hate evil so much?”

“I just pretended not to see evil and passed by, though.”

“If I clumsily touched those one or two scoundrels and left, those people would eventually face retaliation, so aren’t you just leaving for that reason?”

He had read my thoughts exactly. If one were to intervene, one had to uproot them completely. Right now was not a situation where I had the leeway to do so.

“This journey is for sightseeing in Lord Soma’s world.”

“It displeases me that such scoundrels seem to be my world.”

“You misunderstand.”

“Then let us clear up the misunderstanding before we go.”

He gave an order to Cheongmyeon with a glance. At that, Cheongmyeon strode toward the dark-path thugs.

Having heard our conversation earlier, he would go and clean up even their leader thoroughly. Of course, it wouldn’t be long before new dark-path thugs moved in.

When I signaled I An with my eyes to go together, she moved quickly.

“I shall go as well.”

I An followed behind Cheongmyeon.

Geugak Soma leaped lightly and sat on the carriage roof.

“This is the perfect spot to watch.”

I, too, leaped to his side and sat beside him.

“Did you enjoy your meal?”

“I ate roughly.”

“Watching you during the journey, you do not seem to eat well.”

“I have no joy in eating.”

“Is it because of the mask?”

A slightly displeased look flashed across his face. Talk of the mask always made him flinch. But I brought up the mask without hesitation.

To remove that, I would have to pull the mask that had become a myth back into reality.

“I have never liked eating.”

“It must be because of the mask. If you remove it, I shall treat you to a magnificent meal.”

Geugak Soma tilted his head for the first time. I read this emotion from the gaze within the eye holes.

Just what are you? What is your identity that you grate on my nerves like this?

Then, a scream was heard from afar.

Our gazes naturally turned toward I An and Cheongmyeon in the distance.

The two had soundly beaten the dark-path thugs and were dragging them somewhere with the thugs leading the way.

Watching that sight, Geugak Soma spoke.

“The Second Young Master said before, didn’t he? That the only time evil looks magnificent is when it suppresses other evil. How is it? Do I look magnificent now?”

“You are magnificent.”

“But why do I not feel good?”

“It must be because it feels like strips of my own flesh are being torn away.”

Even at my joke, Geugak Soma laughed. He asked the question he had asked before again.

“Why do you hate evil so much?”

Since he had asked twice, I had to give some answer. After a brief pause, I said to him.

“There are people who deliberately push the person in front of them off a cliff while walking on the road. If they want to go first, they could simply walk faster and pass by the side. I am not saying to help someone who has collapsed from exhaustion. Just go on your own way—why push them? When I see such scoundrels, I want to push them too. That is why I intend to push them all away.”

“Then I suppose I would want to push them too.”

The two eyes within the mask holes were gleaming coldly.

Soma, how many people have you pushed into misfortune in your life until now?

I smiled and said to him.

“So please do not walk in front of me as much as possible.”

“Then must I stand behind the Second Young Master?”

“Then let us walk side by side. I think walking while maintaining the tension that either of us could push the other at any moment is not bad either.”

This is precisely the relationship I desire with him.

Geugak Soma laughed loudly.

“Very well. If it is the Second Young Master, you could be a good companion.”

Shortly after, I An and Cheongmyeon, having finished cleaning everything up, returned to the carriage carrying a faint scent of blood.

The pupils of I An, who looked up at us sitting side by side on the carriage roof, trembled slightly. No matter how much she trusted me, it would not be easy to completely shake off the fear inspired by a being like Geugak Soma.

The carriage set off again.

After racing for several days like that, the carriage arrived at a manor.

A man was waiting in the courtyard.

I put on the white mask I wore at my waist and got off following Geugak Soma. I would reveal my face after seeing how the return situation unfolded.

“It has been a while, Lord Demon Venerable.”

Geugak Soma nodded with cold eyes.

I remembered who the man was.

Im Ho, the tycoon of Ghost Prefecture.

Originally, this place, Ghost Prefecture, had been the region under Geugak Soma’s charge.

The Eight Demon Venerables were each responsible for businesses in their respective regions, intervening in or directly operating all manner of profit-making ventures such as merchant groups, escort agencies, blacksmith shops, brothels, taverns, and so on.

Even Hyeolcheon Doma, for instance, seemed to be leisurely reading books, but he too had a region he was responsible for.

Im Ho was the head of the Great Lake Merchant Group, which represented Ghost Prefecture, and a figure who actively supported Geugak Soma.

“You seem to have lost some weight.”

Im Ho bowed at the curt remark.

“I have lost some weight recently. Thank you very much for noticing.”

Geugak Soma tapped his shoulder once as if in encouragement.

“Thank you.”

Im Ho felt fearful yet humbled.

Then, I An sent me a transmission.

—Did you see that tap on the shoulder just now? Not a slap on the cheek or shattering the shoulder? I thought he was a different person.

—If Geugak Soma were merely a mad slaughterer as the rumors say, he would not even belong among the Eight Demon Venerables. Watch carefully how Geugak Soma treats and handles people. Whether as something to learn from or a lesson by negative example, it will surely help you in operating the Ghost Shadow Unit in the future.

—Yes, young master.

There are many faces within that mask. There must be faces I have seen before, and new faces that emerge upon meeting the present me.

Im Ho asked Geugak Soma.

“Shall we start with a meal?”

“No need. Let us see the guild leaders first.”

“Yes, then please come this way.”

Im Ho did not ask about me at all.

He must have guessed that I was no ordinary servant since I had gotten off the carriage together with him, but he did not dare ask to be introduced.

From that alone, one could tell how much Im Ho feared Geugak Soma.

Of course, that did not mean Im Ho was a man lacking guts or a fearful one. He was a man who ran a merchant company in partnership with the Demonic Cult. Among the merchants, Geugak Soma might very well be just that.

Im Ho guided us to the great hall.

Inside, more than twenty people were waiting; they were all figures who ran large and small merchant companies in this Ghost Prefecture.

As Geugak Soma entered, they all prostrated themselves and bowed.

“We greet the great Demon Venerable.”

Geugak Soma sat in the seat of honor.

Then, one of the merchant leaders who had been prostrated stood up and went before Geugak Soma.

He respectfully presented the ledger he had brought to Geugak Soma. It seemed to be a ritual they always performed when Soma visited.

Geugak Soma slowly flipped through the ledger.

Watching that sight, I An sent another transmission.

—I did not know Geugak Soma could read ledgers.

—He cannot read them.

—What?

—Cheongmyeon standing behind him is telling him the contents via transmission. He is the smartest person in the Valley of Villains, and the brightest at calculations. It is also the reason why he is kept as his right-hand man.

—Ah, I see.

Geugak Soma was well-informed not only about the ledgers but also about the personal affairs of the merchant leaders. He apologized for being unable to attend a son’s wedding, and expressed sorrowful condolences to someone who had suffered a bereavement.

—Does Cheongmyeon inform him of these things too?

—No. This is Geugak Soma remembering everything he has been told.

—Really? He says such things because he remembers all of that? Geugak Soma?

In my past life, there was one thing I had realized.

It was that real villains are meticulous.

Though it could not be generalized, the great villains I had experienced were meticulous. Their observation was good, and their memory was good too.

On the other hand, the great heroes of the righteous faction I met were not meticulous. They forgot easily. They regarded it as a natural carelessness born from valuing great righteousness, but I thought they had already lost to the villains there. Lofty principles floating like clouds could not overcome such finger-counting meticulousness.

In the meantime, Geugak Soma finished brief one-on-one meetings with each of the merchant leaders.

The merchant leaders were deeply moved that Geugak Soma remembered and mentioned their daily affairs, even as they feared him.

—A short meeting held once a year would keep those people from making any moves for ten or twenty years. If Geugak Soma asks after their families with a smile, how could they possibly dare to betray him?

—It is frightening.

—Yes, this is precisely what is frightening.

After the greetings with the merchant leaders ended, Im Ho stepped forward as their representative and reported.

“As you saw, our income has decreased greatly over the past two years. The reason is the Baekgye Merchant Group.”

The newly established Baekgye Merchant Group in Ghost Prefecture had grown explosively faster than any other merchant group. Behind it was the active support of the Evil Path Alliance.

This matter began with the incident where Geugak Soma massacred the martial artists of the Evil Path Alliance.

The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult did not officially apologize or compensate the Evil Path Alliance for that matter. Instead, they placated the Evil Path Alliance by placing Geugak Soma under confinement.

But the Evil Path Alliance considered that insufficient.

The Evil Path Alliance had established the Baekgye Merchant Group in Geugak Soma’s territory on the level of punitive measures, openly stealing his income.

From Geugak Soma’s perspective, as he was being punished by the Heavenly Demon, he could not start a fight, so for the past two years he had no choice but to watch them expand their territory. In the meantime, the dissatisfaction of his affiliated merchant groups had continued to grow.

As if he already knew well about the Baekgye Merchant Group issue, Geugak Soma did not mention it separately.

“Now that I am able to move directly, do not worry too much.”

Only then did the merchant leaders’ faces brighten, putting on joyful expressions.

With those words, Geugak Soma ended the meeting. He was someone who knew better than anyone that such gatherings became targets for enemies. Meetings were secret and brief.

Geugak Soma, having boarded the carriage, spoke to Cheongmyeon.

“We are going to the Baekgye Merchant Group.”

“Yes!”

The carriage began to race.

I spoke casually to Geugak Soma.

“You were kind to the merchant leaders. I thought you were Affectionate Soma, not Geugak Soma.”

“Are they not the ones who earn money for me? People who will earn much more money with just a kind word—why should I treat them coldly?”

They were words that formed a stark contrast with the dark-path thugs who had been robbing merchants and causing trouble earlier.

Real villains are people like this. Tightening the noose around someone while smiling, making them continue to suffer in a pleasant atmosphere.

“Their very name is wrong from the start. It must be the ‘Gye’ of ‘punish one, warn a hundred.’ At the Baekgye Merchant Group, I intend to show them why I bear the epithet Geugak.”

When I showed no reaction, he spoke again.

“Are you not worried? Do you think I am unable to kill them all? That I wouldn’t dare kill them now that my confinement has just been lifted? Is that what you think?”

“No. I can feel that Lord Soma is very angry right now. The air inside the carriage is already different.”

“But why do you not stop me? If an incident breaks out at the Baekgye Merchant Group, the Second Young Master must take responsibility, you know?”

“Lord Soma, please stop the carriage. Vent your anger and go somewhere else.”

“I refuse.”

“I anticipated this. That is why I have been contemplating how to stop you.”

“You will have to contemplate a great deal.”

Finishing those words, Geugak Soma leaned against the carriage and closed his eyes.

He let out a deliberately audible sigh for me to hear, but my gaze looking outside the carriage was incomparably calm. Though the night was deep, the martial world was not asleep.
